------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 100/123: /src/pages/admin/UsersPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import { Users, ShieldCheck, Briefcase, UserCircle, UserCheck, Store, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock, Ban, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';

const roleIcons = {
  admin: ShieldCheck,
  entrepreneur: Briefcase,
  merchant: Store,
  citizen: UserCircle,
};

const roleColors = {
  admin: 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive',
  entrepreneur: 'bg-primary/10 text-primary',
  merchant: 'bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-600',
  citizen: 'bg-accent/10 text-accent',
};

const roleLabels = {
  admin: 'Admin',
  entrepreneur: 'Annonceur',
  merchant: 'Commerçant',
  citizen: 'Citoyen',
};

const statusConfig = {
  pending_validation: { label: 'En attente', color: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600', icon: Clock },
  active: { label: 'Actif', color: 'bg-accent/10 text-accent', icon: CheckCircle2 },
  rejected: { label: 'Refusé', color: 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive', icon: XCircle },
  blocked: { label: 'Bloqué', color: 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive', icon: Ban },
};

export default function UsersPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');

  const { data: users = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['all-users'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.User.list('-created_date', 200),
  });

  const approveUser = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (userId) => {
      const user = users.find((u) => u.id === userId);
      const targetRole = user.requested_role || user.role || 'citizen';
      const updates = { account_status: 'active', role: targetRole };
      // Generate affiliation code for merchants if not already set
      if (targetRole === 'merchant' && !user.affiliation_code) {
        const prefix = ((user.company_name || user.first_name || 'COM').slice(0, 3)).toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, 'C');
        updates.affiliation_code = `${prefix}${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
      }
      await base44.entities.User.update(userId, updates);
      // Ensure a publicly-readable ReferralCode exists for the merchant
      if (targetRole === 'merchant' && updates.affiliation_code) {
        try {
          const existing = await base44.entities.ReferralCode.filter({ code: updates.affiliation_code });
          if (existing.length === 0) {
            await base44.entities.ReferralCode.create({
              code: updates.affiliation_code,
              owner_id: userId,
              owner_role: 'merchant',
              owner_name: user.company_name || [user.first_name, user.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || user.email,
              status: 'active',
            });
          }
        } catch {}
      }
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['all-users'] });
      toast({ title: 'Compte validé' });
    },
  });

  const rejectUser = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (userId) => base44.entities.User.update(userId, { account_status: 'rejected' }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['all-users'] });
      toast({ title: 'Compte refusé' });
    },
  });

  const sanctionUser = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (userId) => {
      const u = users.find((x) => x.id === userId);
      const count = (u.sanction_count || 0) + 1;
      await base44.entities.User.update(userId, { sanction_count: count });
      try {
        await base44.integrations.Core.SendEmail({
          to: u.email,
          subject: `Notification de sanction #${count} — P-interact`,
          body: `Bonjour,\n\nVous avez reçu une notification de sanction #${count} de la part de l'administrateur P-interact pour non-respect des consignes du programme.\n\nAprès 3 notifications, votre compte sera bloqué.\n\nCordialement,\nL'équipe P-interact`,
        });
      } catch {}
      return count;
    },
    onSuccess: (count) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['all-users'] });
      toast({ title: `Sanction envoyée (${count}/3)`, description: count >= 3 ? 'Le compte peut maintenant être bloqué' : 'Notification email envoyée' });
    },
  });

  const blockUser = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (userId) => base44.entities.User.update(userId, { account_status: 'blocked' }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['all-users'] });
      toast({ title: 'Compte bloqué', description: 'L\'utilisateur ne peut plus accéder à la plateforme' });
    },
  });

  const unblockUser = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (userId) => base44.entities.User.update(userId, { account_status: 'active', sanction_count: 0 }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['all-users'] });
      toast({ title: 'Compte réactivé' });
    },
  });

  // Only entrepreneur and merchant need admin validation
  const pendingUsers = users.filter(
    (u) => u.account_status === 'pending_validation' &&
      (u.requested_role === 'entrepreneur' || u.requested_role === 'merchant')
  );
  const pendingCount = pendingUsers.length;
  const blockedUsers = users.filter((u) => u.account_status === 'blocked');
  const blockedCount = blockedUsers.length;
  const filteredUsers = filter === 'pending' ? pendingUsers : filter === 'blocked' ? blockedUsers : users;

  const approveAll = () => {
    pendingUsers.forEach((u) => approveUser.mutate(u.id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users className="w-6 h-6 text-primary" /> Gestion des Utilisateurs
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">
            {users.length} utilisateur(s) · {pendingCount} en attente de validation
          </p>
        </div>
        {pendingCount > 0 && (
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={approveAll} disabled={approveUser.isPending} className="gap-1.5">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-accent" /> Valider tous ({pendingCount})
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Filter tabs */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
        {[
          { key: 'all', label: `Tous (${users.length})` },
          { key: 'pending', label: `En attente (${pendingCount})` },
          { key: 'blocked', label: `Bloqués (${blockedCount})` },
        ].map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.key}
            onClick={() => setFilter(t.key)}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              filter === t.key ? 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground' : 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted/70'
            }`}
          >
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Card className="overflow-hidden">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow className="bg-muted/50">
              <TableHead>Utilisateur</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Rôle</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Statut</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="hidden md:table-cell">Inscrit le</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="text-right">Actions</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {isLoading ? (
              Array(5).fill(0).map((_, i) => (
                <TableRow key={i} className="animate-pulse">
                  <TableCell><div className="h-4 bg-muted rounded w-32" /></TableCell>
                  <TableCell><div className="h-5 bg-muted rounded w-20" /></TableCell>
                  <TableCell><div className="h-5 bg-muted rounded w-20" /></TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="hidden md:table-cell"><div className="h-4 bg-muted rounded w-24" /></TableCell>
                  <TableCell><div className="h-8 bg-muted rounded w-20" /></TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))
            ) : filteredUsers.length === 0 ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={5} className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
                  Aucun utilisateur
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : (
              filteredUsers.map((user) => {
                // For pending users show requested_role, else actual role
                const role = (user.account_status === 'pending_validation' && user.requested_role)
                  ? user.requested_role
                  : (user.role || 'citizen');
                const RoleIcon = roleIcons[role] || UserCircle;
                const status = statusConfig[user.account_status] || statusConfig.active;
                const StatusIcon = status.icon;
                const displayName =
                  [user.first_name, user.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ') ||
                  user.full_name ||
                  'Sans nom';
                return (
                  <TableRow key={user.id} className="hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                    <TableCell>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
                            {(user.first_name?.[0] || user.full_name?.[0] || user.email?.[0] || '?').toUpperCase()}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm font-medium">{displayName}</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{user.email}</p>
                          {user.phone && (
                            <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground/70">{user.phone}</p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <Badge className={`${roleColors[role] || ''} text-[10px] gap-1`}>
                        <RoleIcon className="w-3 h-3" />
                        {roleLabels[role] || role}
                      </Badge>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <Badge className={`${status.color} text-[10px] gap-1`}>
                        <StatusIcon className="w-3 h-3" />
                        {status.label}
                      </Badge>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="hidden md:table-cell text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {user.created_date ? format(new Date(user.created_date), 'dd/MM/yyyy') : '—'}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right">
                      {user.account_status === 'pending_validation' && role !== 'admin' && (
                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="outline"
                            className="h-7 gap-1 text-xs text-accent border-accent/30 hover:bg-accent/10"
                            onClick={() => approveUser.mutate(user.id)}
                            disabled={approveUser.isPending}
                          >
                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Valider
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="outline"
                            className="h-7 gap-1 text-xs text-destructive border-destructive/30 hover:bg-destructive/10"
                            onClick={() => rejectUser.mutate(user.id)}
                            disabled={rejectUser.isPending}
                          >
                            <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Refuser
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {user.account_status === 'active' && role !== 'admin' && (
                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-1 items-center flex-wrap">
                          {(user.sanction_count || 0) > 0 && (
                            <Badge className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 text-[10px] gap-0.5">
                              <AlertTriangle className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> {user.sanction_count}/3
                            </Badge>
                          )}
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="ghost"
                            className="h-7 gap-1 text-xs text-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500/10"
                            onClick={() => sanctionUser.mutate(user.id)}
                            disabled={sanctionUser.isPending}
                            title="Envoyer une notification de sanction"
                          >
                            <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Sanctionner
                          </Button>
                          {(user.sanction_count || 0) >= 3 && (
                            <Button
                              size="sm"
                              variant="outline"
                              className="h-7 gap-1 text-xs text-destructive border-destructive/30 hover:bg-destructive/10"
                              onClick={() => blockUser.mutate(user.id)}
                              disabled={blockUser.isPending}
                            >
                              <Ban className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Bloquer
                            </Button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {user.account_status === 'blocked' && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="outline"
                          className="h-7 gap-1 text-xs text-accent border-accent/30 hover:bg-accent/10"
                          onClick={() => unblockUser.mutate(user.id)}
                          disabled={unblockUser.isPending}
                        >
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Réactiver
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                );
              })
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER 101/123: /src/pages/citizen/CitizenDashboard.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import StatCard from '@/components/shared/StatCard';
import { useTranslation } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { Eye, Wallet, Users, Zap, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

export default function CitizenDashboard() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: wallets = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-wallet'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: user?.id }),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const { data: interactions = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-interactions'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Interaction.filter({ user_id: user?.id }, '-created_date', 10),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const { data: campaigns = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['active-campaigns'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Campaign.filter({ status: 'active' }, '-created_date', 5),
  });

  const wallet = wallets[0];

  const container = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0 },
    show: { opacity: 1, transition: { staggerChildren: 0.08 } }
  };
  const item = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 },
    show: { opacity: 1, y: 0 }
  };

  return (
    <motion.div variants={container} initial="hidden" animate="show" className="space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <motion.div variants={item}>
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-heading font-bold">
          {t('dash.welcome')}{user?.full_name ? `, ${user.full_name}` : ''} 👋
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">{t('dash.tagline')}</p>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <motion.div variants={item} className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <StatCard
          title={t('dash.balance')}
          value={`${(wallet?.balance_dt || 0).toFixed(2)} DT`}
          icon={Wallet}
          variant="primary"
          trend={12}
          trendLabel={t('dash.this_week')}
        />
        <StatCard
          title={t('dash.total_earned')}
          value={`${(wallet?.total_earned_dt || 0).toFixed(2)} DT`}
          icon={Zap}
          variant="accent"
        />
        <StatCard
          title={t('dash.interactions')}
          value={wallet?.total_interactions || 0}
          icon={Eye}
        />
        <StatCard
          title={t('dash.vouchers')}
          value={`${(wallet?.referral_earnings_dt || 0).toFixed(2)} DT`}
          icon={Users}
          variant="warning"
        />
      </motion.div>

      {/* Available Campaigns */}
      <motion.div variants={item}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold">{t('dash.available')}</h2>
          <Link to="/watch">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="gap-1 text-primary">
              {t('dash.see_all')} <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {campaigns.length === 0 ? (
            <Card className="col-span-full p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
              {t('dash.no_campaigns')}
            </Card>
          ) : (
            campaigns.slice(0, 3).map((campaign) => (
              <Card key={campaign.id} className="overflow-hidden group hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow duration-300">
                <div className="aspect-video bg-muted relative overflow-hidden">
                  {campaign.thumbnail_url ? (
                    <img src={campaign.thumbnail_url} alt={campaign.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/20 to-accent/20">
                      <Eye className="w-10 h-10 text-primary/40" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <Badge className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-accent text-accent-foreground">
                    {campaign.gain_per_view_dt?.toFixed(3) || '0.050'} DT
                  </Badge>
                </div>
                <div className="p-4">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-sm">{campaign.title}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{campaign.brand_name}</p>
                  <Link to="/watch" className="mt-3 block">
                    <Button size="sm" className="w-full gap-2">
                      <Eye className="w-4 h-4" /> {t('dash.watch_earn')}
                    </Button>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </Card>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Recent Activity */}
      <motion.div variants={item}>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold mb-4">{t('dash.recent')}</h2>
        <Card className="divide-y divide-border">
          {interactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-6 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              {t('dash.no_activity')}
            </div>
          ) : (
            interactions.slice(0, 5).map((interaction) => (
              <div key={interaction.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center ${
                    interaction.pow_validated ? 'bg-accent/10' : 'bg-muted'
                  }`}>
                    <Eye className={`w-4 h-4 ${interaction.pow_validated ? 'text-accent' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">
                      {interaction.pool_type === 'pool_a' ? t('dash.phygital') : t('dash.digital')}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {t('dash.score')}: {interaction.attention_score || 0}/100
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent">+{(interaction.gain_earned_dt || 0).toFixed(3)} DT</p>
                  <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px]">
                    {interaction.pow_validated ? t('dash.validated') : t('dash.in_progress')}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </Card>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

FICHIER 102/123: /src/pages/citizen/CitizenVouchersPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Ticket, QrCode, CheckCircle2, Coins, Info } from 'lucide-react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import QRCodeCard from '@/components/shared/QRCodeCard';

// Simple barcode visual from code characters
function Barcode({ code }) {
  const bars = code.split('').map((char, i) => {
    const width = (char.charCodeAt(0) % 4) + 1;
    const isBlack = i % 2 === 0;
    return (
      <div key={i} style={{ width: `${width * 2}px` }} className={isBlack ? 'bg-black h-full' : 'bg-white h-full'} />
    );
  });
  return (
    <div className="flex items-end h-12 gap-px bg-white p-1 rounded justify-center">
      {bars}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function CitizenVouchersPage() {
  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: vouchers = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-extracted-vouchers'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ citizen_id: user?.id }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  // Only extracted vouchers are active — used/returned are destroyed from citizen account
  const activeVouchers = vouchers.filter((v) => v.status === 'extracted');
  const usedVouchers = vouchers.filter((v) => v.status === 'used' || v.status === 'returned');
  const totalRealValue = activeVouchers.reduce((s, v) => s + (v.real_value_dt || (v.value_dt || 0) * 0.9), 0);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <Ticket className="w-6 h-6 text-accent" /> Bons Extraits
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Vos Bons Cadeaux extraits — présentez le QR chez votre commerçant</p>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Ticket className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{activeVouchers.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Bons actifs</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Coins className="w-5 h-5 text-accent mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{totalRealValue.toFixed(2)}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Valeur réelle (DT)</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-primary mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{usedVouchers.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Bons utilisés</p>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Active Vouchers */}
      {activeVouchers.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">
          <Ticket className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 text-muted-foreground/30" />
          <p className="text-sm">Aucun Bon Cadeau extrait. Accumulez des gains dans votre porte-feuille et extrayez un bon.</p>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {activeVouchers.map((v) => {
            const realValue = v.real_value_dt || (v.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
            return (
              <Card key={v.id} className="overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-accent/5 to-primary/5 border-b border-border">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{v.title}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">{v.code}</p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 text-[10px]">Extrait</Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
                  {/* Real value notification */}
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-accent/10 border border-accent/30 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-accent flex-shrink-0" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Valeur d'achat réelle</p>
                      <p className="text-lg font-bold text-accent">{realValue.toFixed(2)} DT</p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
                        Valeur nominale: {(v.value_dt || 0).toFixed(2)} DT − 10% commerçant
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {/* QR + Barcode */}
                  <Dialog>
                    <DialogTrigger asChild>
                      <Button className="w-full gap-2">
                        <QrCode className="w-4 h-4" /> Afficher le Bon
                      </Button>
                    </DialogTrigger>
                    <DialogContent className="max-w-sm">
                      <DialogHeader>
                        <DialogTitle className="text-sm">Bon Cadeau — {v.code}</DialogTitle>
                      </DialogHeader>
                      <div className="space-y-4">
                        <QRCodeCard
                          value={v.code}
                          size={180}
                          title="Bon Cadeau"
                          subtitle={`Valeur réelle: ${realValue.toFixed(2)} DT`}
                          fileName={`bon-${v.code}`}
                        />
                        <div className="space-y-1">
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground text-center">Code à barres</p>
                          <Barcode code={v.code} />
                          <p className="text-xs text-center font-mono mt-1">{v.code}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-accent/10 border border-accent/30 text-center">
                          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-accent">{realValue.toFixed(2)} DT</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Valeur d'achat réelle (−10%)</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </DialogContent>
                  </Dialog>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Used vouchers history */}
      {usedVouchers.length > 0 && (
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold mb-3">Historique des Bons Utilisés</h2>
          <Card className="divide-y divide-border">
            {usedVouchers.map((v) => (
              <div key={v.id} className="p-3 flex items-center justify-between opacity-60">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{v.title}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">{v.code}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{(v.value_dt || 0).toFixed(2)} DT</p>
                  <Badge className="bg-accent/10 text-accent text-[10px]">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Utilisé
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 103/123: /src/pages/citizen/WalletPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Wallet, ShieldCheck, Clock, Coins, Gift, Building2, Bell } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';
import { formatDT } from '@/lib/format';

export default function WalletPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [extractOpen, setExtractOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSegment, setSelectedSegment] = useState(null);
  const [extractValue, setExtractValue] = useState(25);
  const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(false);

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: interactions = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-interactions-all'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Interaction.filter({ user_id: user?.id }, '-created_date', 200),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const { data: vouchers = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-extracted-vouchers'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ citizen_id: user?.id }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  // Issued voucher configs from advertisers (available Bon Cadeau values)
  const { data: issuedConfigs = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['issued-voucher-configs'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ status: 'issued' }, '-created_date', 200),
  });

  const { data: myWallet } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-wallet'],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const wallets = await base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: user?.id });
      return wallets[0] || null;
    },
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  // Build segments grouped by advertiser
  const advertiserMap = {};
  interactions.forEach((i) => {
    const advId = i.advertiser_id;
    if (!advId) return;
    if (!advertiserMap[advId]) {
      advertiserMap[advId] = { id: advId, name: i.advertiser_name || 'Annonceur', earned: 0 };
    }
    advertiserMap[advId].earned += i.gain_earned_dt || 0;
  });

  vouchers.forEach((v) => {
    if (advertiserMap[v.entrepreneur_id]) {
      advertiserMap[v.entrepreneur_id].earned -= v.value_dt || 0;
    }
  });

  const segments = Object.values(advertiserMap).map((a) => {
    const availableValues = [...new Set(issuedConfigs.filter((c) => c.entrepreneur_id === a.id).map((c) => c.value_dt))].sort((x, y) => x - y);
    return { ...a, balance: Math.max(0, a.earned), availableValues };
  });

  const totalBalance = segments.reduce((s, seg) => s + seg.balance, 0);
  const totalEarned = interactions.reduce((s, i) => s + (i.gain_earned_dt || 0), 0);

  const extractVoucher = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const code = 'BC-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8).toUpperCase();
      return base44.entities.Voucher.create({
        title: `Bon Cadeau ${selectedSegment.name}`,
        code,
        entrepreneur_id: selectedSegment.id,
        advertiser_name: selectedSegment.name,
        citizen_id: user.id,
        value_dt: extractValue,
        real_value_dt: extractValue * 0.9,
        remaining_value_dt: extractValue * 0.9,
        status: 'extracted',
        face_match_verified: true,
      });
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-extracted-vouchers'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-interactions-all'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['issued-voucher-configs'] });
      toast({ title: 'Bon Cadeau extrait', description: `${extractValue} DT — validation Face Match réussie` });
      setExtractOpen(false);
      setVerifying(false);
      setSelectedSegment(null);
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur', description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });
      setVerifying(false);
    },
  });

  const handleExtract = () => {
    setVerifying(true);
    setTimeout(() => extractVoucher.mutate(), 1500);
  };

  const openExtract = (segment) => {
    setSelectedSegment(segment);
    const vals = segment.availableValues.filter((v) => v <= segment.balance);
    setExtractValue(vals[0] || segment.availableValues[0] || 25);
    setExtractOpen(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <Wallet className="w-6 h-6 text-primary" /> Mes Porte-Feuilles
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Gains segmentés par annonceur — extrayez vos Bons Cadeaux</p>
      </div>

      {/* Total Balance Card */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}>
        <Card className="bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/10 via-card to-accent/10 p-6 md:p-8">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-primary/10">
              <Wallet className="w-6 h-6 text-primary" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Solde Total Disponible</p>
              <p className="text-4xl font-heading font-bold">{formatDT(totalBalance)} <span className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">DT</span></p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-card/60 backdrop-blur-sm">
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Total Gagné</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-bold mt-1">{formatDT(totalEarned)} DT</p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-card/60 backdrop-blur-sm">
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Annonceurs</p>
              <p className="text-lg font-bold mt-1">{segments.length}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Threshold notification */}
      {(() => {
        const readySegments = segments.filter((s) => s.availableValues.some((v) => v <= s.balance));
        return readySegments.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-accent/10 border border-accent/30 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-accent/20 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
              <Bell className="w-5 h-5 text-accent" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent">Bon Cadeau déclenché !</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {readySegments.map((s) => s.name).join(', ')} — solde suffisant pour extraire un Bon Cadeau.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null;
      })()}

      {/* Segmented Wallets */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" /> Porte-Feuilles par Annonceur
        </h2>
        {segments.length === 0 ? (
          <Card className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            <Wallet className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground/30" />
            Aucun gain pour le moment. Regardez des campagnes pour commencer à gagner.
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {segments.map((seg) => {
              const extractable = seg.availableValues.filter((v) => v <= seg.balance);
              return (
                <Card key={seg.id} className="p-5">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <Building2 className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{seg.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Annonceur</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px]">Segment</Badge>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{formatDT(seg.balance)} <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">DT</span></p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                    Total gagné: {formatDT(seg.earned + (vouchers.filter((v) => v.entrepreneur_id === seg.id).reduce((s, v) => s + (v.value_dt || 0), 0)))} DT
                  </p>
                  {extractable.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Bons Cadeaux disponibles:</p>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        {extractable.map((v) => (
                          <Button key={v} size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1 h-8" onClick={() => { setSelectedSegment(seg); setExtractValue(v); setExtractOpen(true); }}>
                            <Gift className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {v} DT
                          </Button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : seg.availableValues.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="mt-3 p-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 text-center">
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
                        Seuil minimum: {Math.min(...seg.availableValues)} DT
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="mt-3 p-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 text-center">
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Annonceur sans Bon Cadeau configuré</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </Card>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Extraction Dialog */}
      <Dialog open={extractOpen} onOpenChange={setExtractOpen}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Gift className="w-5 h-5 text-accent" /> Extraire un Bon Cadeau
            </DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          {selectedSegment && (
            <div className="space-y-4 mt-2">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 border border-border">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Annonceur</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{selectedSegment.name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">Solde disponible: {formatDT(selectedSegment.balance)} DT</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-2">Valeur du Bon Cadeau</p>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  {selectedSegment.availableValues.map((v) => (
                    <button
                      key={v}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={v > selectedSegment.balance}
                      onClick={() => setExtractValue(v)}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-center transition-all ${
                        extractValue === v ? 'border-accent bg-accent/10' : 'border-border hover:border-accent/40'
                      } ${v > selectedSegment.balance ? 'opacity-40 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}`}
                    >
                      <p className="text-lg font-bold">{v} DT</p>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/20 p-3 rounded-lg">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 flex-shrink-0" />
                Validation Face Match requise pour l'extraction du Bon Cadeau.
              </div>
              <Button className="w-full gap-2" onClick={handleExtract} disabled={verifying || extractVoucher.isPending}>
                {verifying || extractVoucher.isPending ? (
                  <><div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-primary-foreground/30 border-t-primary-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" /> Vérification Face Match...</>
                ) : (
                  <><ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" /> Vérifier & Extraire</>
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Transaction History */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold mb-3">Historique des Gains</h2>
        <Card className="divide-y divide-border">
          {interactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              <Clock className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground/30" />
              Aucune transaction
            </div>
          ) : (
            interactions.slice(0, 30).map((tx) => (
              <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-accent/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Coins className="w-4 h-4 text-accent" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{tx.advertiser_name || 'Campagne'}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {tx.created_date ? format(new Date(tx.created_date), 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '—'}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent">+{formatDT(tx.gain_earned_dt)} DT</p>
                  <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px] px-1.5">
                    {tx.attention_score || 0}/100
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 104/123: /src/pages/citizen/WatchEarn.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Progress } from '@/components/ui/progress';
import { Eye, EyeOff, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Coins, Shield, ChevronRight, Video, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useTranslation } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';
import { formatDT } from '@/lib/format';

const EMOJIS = ['👍', '❤️', '😮', '🔥', '🎉', '😂'];

export default function WatchEarn() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [selectedCampaign, setSelectedCampaign] = useState(null);
  const [watching, setWatching] = useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [attentionActive, setAttentionActive] = useState(true);
  const [tabSwitches, setTabSwitches] = useState(0);
  const [completed, setCompleted] = useState(false);
  const [reaction, setReaction] = useState(null);
  const [collected, setCollected] = useState(false);
  const [actualGain, setActualGain] = useState(null);
  const videoRef = useRef(null);
  const timerRef = useRef(null);
  const completedRef = useRef(false);
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: campaigns = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['active-campaigns'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Campaign.filter({ status: 'active' }, '-created_date', 20),
  });

  // Fetch user's completed interactions to hide already-watched campaigns
  const { data: myInteractions = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-interactions'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Interaction.filter({ user_id: user?.id }, '-created_date', 100),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const watchedCampaignIds = new Set(
    myInteractions.filter((i) => i.status === 'completed').map((i) => i.campaign_id)
  );
  const availableCampaigns = campaigns.filter((c) => !watchedCampaignIds.has(c.id));

  // Anti-tab-switching detection (Proof-of-Watch)
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden && watching && !completed) {
        setAttentionActive(false);
        setTabSwitches((prev) => prev + 1);
        if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.pause();
        if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
      } else if (!document.hidden && watching && !completed) {
        setAttentionActive(true);
        if (videoRef.current && selectedCampaign?.video_url) videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
        else startTimerFallback();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibility);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibility);
  }, [watching, completed, selectedCampaign]);

  const startTimerFallback = useCallback(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    const duration = (selectedCampaign?.video_duration_sec || 30) * 1000;
    const step = 100 / (duration / 100);
    timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress((prev) => {
        if (prev >= 100) {
          clearInterval(timerRef.current);
          handleVideoComplete();
          return 100;
        }
        return prev + step;
      });
    }, 100);
  }, [selectedCampaign]);

  const handleTimeUpdate = () => {
    const v = videoRef.current;
    if (!v || !v.duration || completedRef.current) return;
    const pct = (v.currentTime / v.duration) * 100;
    setProgress(pct);
    if (pct >= 99.5) handleVideoComplete();
  };

  const handleVideoComplete = () => {
    if (completedRef.current) return;
    completedRef.current = true;
    if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.pause();
    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    setProgress(100);
    setCompleted(true);
    setWatching(false);
  };

  const createInteraction = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (data) => {
      const interaction = await base44.entities.Interaction.create(data);
      const netGain = data.gain_earned_dt;
      const grossGain = netGain / 0.95;
      const watchCommission = grossGain * 0.05;

      // Credit citizen (net gain) + track program watch commission
      const freshWallets = await base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: data.user_id });
      const wallet = freshWallets[0];
      if (wallet) {
        await base44.entities.Wallet.update(wallet.id, {
          balance_dt: (wallet.balance_dt || 0) + netGain,
          total_earned_dt: (wallet.total_earned_dt || 0) + netGain,
          total_interactions: (wallet.total_interactions || 0) + 1,
          platform_commission_dt: (wallet.platform_commission_dt || 0) + watchCommission,
        });
      } else {
        await base44.entities.Wallet.create({
          user_id: data.user_id,
          balance_dt: netGain,
          total_earned_dt: netGain,
          total_interactions: 1,
          platform_commission_dt: watchCommission,
        });
      }

      // Credit affiliated merchant: 5% of citizen net gain, minus 5% program commission
      const referrals = await base44.entities.Referral.filter({ referred_id: data.user_id, status: 'active' });
      if (referrals.length > 0) {
        const referral = referrals[0];
        const merchantGrossGain = netGain * 0.05;
        const affiliationCommission = merchantGrossGain * 0.05;
        const merchantNetGain = merchantGrossGain - affiliationCommission;

        const merchantWallets = await base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: referral.referrer_id });
        if (merchantWallets.length > 0) {
          const mw = merchantWallets[0];
          await base44.entities.Wallet.update(mw.id, {
            balance_dt: (mw.balance_dt || 0) + merchantNetGain,
            affiliation_earnings_dt: (mw.affiliation_earnings_dt || 0) + merchantGrossGain,
            total_earned_dt: (mw.total_earned_dt || 0) + merchantNetGain,
            platform_commission_dt: (mw.platform_commission_dt || 0) + affiliationCommission,
          });
        } else {
          await base44.entities.Wallet.create({
            user_id: referral.referrer_id,
            balance_dt: merchantNetGain,
            affiliation_earnings_dt: merchantGrossGain,
            total_earned_dt: merchantNetGain,
            platform_commission_dt: affiliationCommission,
          });
        }
      }
      return interaction;
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-interactions'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-interactions-all'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-wallet'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['active-campaigns'] });
      setCollected(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setSelectedCampaign(null);
        setCompleted(false);
        setCollected(false);
        setReaction(null);
      }, 2200);
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur de collecte', description: err.message || 'Erreur inconnue', variant: 'destructive' });
      setCollected(false);
    },
  });

  const startWatching = (campaign) => {
    setSelectedCampaign(campaign);
    setWatching(true);
    setProgress(0);
    setTabSwitches(0);
    setCompleted(false);
    setReaction(null);
    setCollected(false);
    setAttentionActive(true);
    completedRef.current = false;
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (watching && selectedCampaign && !selectedCampaign.video_url) {
      setTimeout(startTimerFallback, 400);
    }
  }, [watching, selectedCampaign, startTimerFallback]);

  const PLATFORM_COMMISSION_RATE = 0.05;

  const handleCollect = async () => {
    if (!selectedCampaign || !user || collected || createInteraction.isPending) return;
    let grossGain = selectedCampaign.gain_per_view_dt || 0.05;
    // Degressive Pool B distribution based on interaction count
    const PACK_PB_FALLBACK = { eclat: 0.000349, expansion: 0.000174, prestige: 0.000116, absolu: 0.000087 };
    if (selectedCampaign.interaction_category_id) {
      try {
        const cats = await base44.entities.InteractionCategory.filter({ id: selectedCampaign.interaction_category_id });
        const cat = cats[0];
        if (cat) {
          const pbCoeff = (cat.pb_coefficient || PACK_PB_FALLBACK[cat.code] || 0.000087) / 100;
          const isDigital = selectedCampaign.campaign_type === 'digital';
          const poolBMass = isDigital
            ? (selectedCampaign.budget_dt || 0)
            : (selectedCampaign.budget_dt || 0) * 0.6;
          const existing = await base44.entities.Interaction.filter({ campaign_id: selectedCampaign.id, status: 'completed' });
          const previousGrossSum = existing.reduce((s, i) => s + (i.gain_earned_dt || 0) / 0.95, 0);
          const remainingMass = Math.max(0, poolBMass - previousGrossSum);
          grossGain = remainingMass * pbCoeff;
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fallback to campaign gain */ }
    }
    const netGain = grossGain - grossGain * PLATFORM_COMMISSION_RATE;
    setActualGain(netGain);

    createInteraction.mutate({
      campaign_id: selectedCampaign.id,
      user_id: user.id,
      advertiser_id: selectedCampaign.created_by_id,
      advertiser_name: selectedCampaign.brand_name,
      pool_type: 'pool_b',
      gain_earned_dt: netGain,
      watch_duration_sec: selectedCampaign.video_duration_sec || 30,
      completion_percent: 100,
      attention_score: Math.max(0, 100 - tabSwitches * 20),
      pow_validated: tabSwitches <= 1,
      tab_switches: tabSwitches,
      interaction_type: 'digital_view',
      status: 'completed',
      device_type: /Mobi/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? 'mobile' : 'desktop',
    });
  };

  const netGain = formatDT((selectedCampaign?.gain_per_view_dt || 0.05) * 0.95);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{t('watch.title')}</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">{t('watch.subtitle')}</p>
      </div>

      {/* Watching / Completed / Collected Modal */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {selectedCampaign && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          >
            <motion.div
              initial={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0 }}
              className="w-full max-w-lg"
            >
              <Card className="overflow-hidden">
                {/* VIDEO AREA — only while watching */}
                {watching && !completed && !collected && (
                  <div className="aspect-video bg-black relative flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                    {selectedCampaign.video_url ? (
                      <video
                        ref={videoRef}
                        src={selectedCampaign.video_url}
                        className="w-full h-full object-contain"
                        autoPlay
                        playsInline
                        poster={selectedCampaign.thumbnail_url}
                        onTimeUpdate={handleTimeUpdate}
                        onEnded={handleVideoComplete}
                        onError={startTimerFallback}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        {selectedCampaign.thumbnail_url ? (
                          <img src={selectedCampaign.thumbnail_url} className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-60" alt="" />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="w-full h-full bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/30 to-accent/30" />
                        )}
                        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2">
                          <Video className="w-10 h-10 text-white/40" />
                          <p className="text-white/50 text-xs">{t('watch.video_unavailable')}</p>
                        </div>
                      </>
                    )}

                    {/* Attention Indicator */}
                    <div className={cn(
                      'absolute top-3 right-3 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium',
                      attentionActive
                        ? 'bg-accent/20 text-accent backdrop-blur-sm'
                        : 'bg-destructive/20 text-destructive backdrop-blur-sm'
                    )}>
                      {attentionActive ? (
                        <><Eye className="w-3 h-3" /> {t('watch.attention_active')}</>
                      ) : (
                        <><EyeOff className="w-3 h-3" /> {t('watch.attention_lost')}</>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* PoW Badge */}
                    <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm text-white/80 text-xs">
                      <Shield className="w-3 h-3" /> {t('watch.pow')}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* PROGRESS — only while watching */}
                {watching && !completed && !collected && (
                  <div className="p-5 space-y-3">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-2">
                        <span className="font-medium">{selectedCampaign.title}</span>
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground">{Math.round(progress)}%</span>
                      </div>
                      <Progress value={progress} className="h-2" />
                    </div>
                    {tabSwitches > 0 && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-amber-600 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg">
                        <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />
                        {tabSwitches} {t('watch.tab_switches')}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {t('dash.score')}: {Math.max(0, 100 - tabSwitches * 20)}/100 • {selectedCampaign.brand_name}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* COMPLETED — reaction + collect (in card body, full width) */}
                {completed && !collected && (
                  <div className="p-6 space-y-5">
                    {!reaction ? (
                      <div className="text-center space-y-4">
                        <motion.div initial={{ scale: 0 }} animate={{ scale: 1 }}>
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-14 h-14 text-accent mx-auto" />
                        </motion.div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-lg font-heading font-bold">{t('watch.react_title')}</p>
                          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">{t('watch.react_subtitle')}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
                          {EMOJIS.map((emoji, i) => (
                            <motion.button
                              key={emoji}
                              initial={{ scale: 0, rotate: -30 }}
                              animate={{ scale: 1, rotate: 0 }}
                              transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
                              whileHover={{ scale: 1.2, y: -4 }}
                              whileTap={{ scale: 0.9 }}
                              onClick={() => setReaction(emoji)}
                              className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-muted hover:bg-accent/10 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl transition-colors"
                            >
                              {emoji}
                            </motion.button>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <div className="text-center space-y-4">
                        <motion.div
                          animate={{ scale: [1, 1.3, 1] }}
                          transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
                          className="text-5xl"
                        >
                          {reaction}
                        </motion.div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-3xl font-heading font-bold text-accent">+{netGain} DT</p>
                          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">{t('watch.gain_validated')}</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{t('watch.commission')}</p>
                        </div>
                        <Button
                          onClick={handleCollect}
                          size="lg"
                          className="w-full gap-2 h-12 text-base"
                          disabled={createInteraction.isPending}
                        >
                          {createInteraction.isPending ? (
                            <><div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-primary-foreground/30 border-t-primary-foreground rounded-full animate-spin" /> {t('watch.collecting')}</>
                          ) : (
                            <><Coins className="w-5 h-5" /> {t('watch.collect_btn')}</>
                          )}
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* COLLECTED — success */}
                {collected && (
                  <div className="p-8 text-center space-y-3">
                    <motion.div initial={{ scale: 0 }} animate={{ scale: 1 }}>
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-16 h-16 text-accent mx-auto" />
                    </motion.div>
                    <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold text-accent">+{formatDT(actualGain || 0)} DT</p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{t('watch.gain_validated')}</p>
                  </div>
                )}
              </Card>
            </motion.div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Campaign Grid */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((i) => (
            <Card key={i} className="animate-pulse">
              <div className="aspect-video bg-muted" />
              <div className="p-4 space-y-2">
                <div className="h-4 bg-muted rounded w-3/4" />
                <div className="h-3 bg-muted rounded w-1/2" />
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : availableCampaigns.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-12 text-center">
          <Sparkles className="w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground/30 mx-auto mb-4" />
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            {campaigns.length === 0 ? t('watch.no_campaigns') : 'Toutes les campagnes disponibles ont été regardées. Revenez plus tard !'}
          </p>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {availableCampaigns.map((campaign, idx) => (
            <motion.div
              key={campaign.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: idx * 0.05 }}
            >
              <Card className="overflow-hidden group hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-primary/5 transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => startWatching(campaign)}>
                <div className="aspect-video bg-muted relative overflow-hidden">
                  {campaign.thumbnail_url ? (
                    <img src={campaign.thumbnail_url} alt={campaign.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/20 to-accent/20">
                      <Eye className="w-8 h-8 text-primary/30" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/60 via-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity" />
                  <Badge className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-accent/90 text-accent-foreground">
                    <Coins className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                    {formatDT((campaign.gain_per_view_dt || 0.05) * 0.95)} DT
                  </Badge>
                  {campaign.video_url && (
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="absolute top-2 left-2 bg-black/40 text-white border-white/20 text-[10px] gap-1">
                      <Video className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> VIDEO
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="p-4">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-sm group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">{campaign.title}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{campaign.brand_name}</p>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {campaign.video_duration_sec || 30}s
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-primary font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                      {t('watch.watch_btn')} <ChevronRight className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Card>
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
