FICHIER 105/123: /src/pages/entrepreneur/AnalyticsPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import StatCard from '@/components/shared/StatCard';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Eye, BarChart3, Target, Clock, Zap, Brain } from 'lucide-react';
import { BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, PieChart, Pie, Cell } from 'recharts';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

const COLORS = ['hsl(250,80%,60%)', 'hsl(168,76%,42%)', 'hsl(32,95%,55%)', 'hsl(340,75%,55%)', 'hsl(200,75%,50%)'];

export default function AnalyticsPage() {
  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: campaigns = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-campaigns'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Campaign.filter({ created_by_id: user?.id }),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const totalInteractions = campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.current_interactions || 0), 0);
  const avgAttention = campaigns.length > 0
    ? campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.attention_score || 0), 0) / campaigns.length
    : 0;
  const avgCompletion = campaigns.length > 0
    ? campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.completion_rate || 0), 0) / campaigns.length
    : 0;

  const campaignBars = campaigns.slice(0, 8).map(c => ({
    name: c.title?.slice(0, 12) || 'N/A',
    interactions: c.current_interactions || 0,
    attention: c.attention_score || 0,
  }));

  const categoryData = Object.entries(
    campaigns.reduce((acc, c) => {
      acc[c.category || 'other'] = (acc[c.category || 'other'] || 0) + (c.current_interactions || 0);
      return acc;
    }, {})
  ).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }));

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">Analytics</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Mesures d'attention et performances de vos campagnes</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <StatCard title="Interactions Totales" value={totalInteractions.toLocaleString()} icon={Eye} variant="primary" />
        <StatCard title="Score Attention Moyen" value={`${avgAttention.toFixed(0)}/100`} icon={Brain} variant="accent" />
        <StatCard title="Taux Complétion" value={`${avgCompletion.toFixed(0)}%`} icon={Target} />
        <StatCard title="Campagnes" value={campaigns.length} icon={BarChart3} variant="warning" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {/* Bar Chart */}
        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} className="lg:col-span-2">
          <Card className="p-5">
            <h3 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <BarChart3 className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" /> Interactions par Campagne
            </h3>
            <div className="h-64">
              {campaignBars.length > 0 ? (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={campaignBars}>
                    <XAxis dataKey="name" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                    <Tooltip contentStyle={{ background: 'hsl(228,25%,11%)', border: 'none', borderRadius: '8px', color: 'white', fontSize: '12px' }} />
                    <Bar dataKey="interactions" fill="hsl(250,80%,60%)" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              ) : (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
                  Aucune donnée disponible
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </Card>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Pie Chart */}
        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.1 }}>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <h3 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-4">Par Catégorie</h3>
            <div className="h-52">
              {categoryData.length > 0 ? (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie data={categoryData} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" cx="50%" cy="50%" outerRadius={80} innerRadius={40}>
                      {categoryData.map((_, idx) => (
                        <Cell key={idx} fill={COLORS[idx % COLORS.length]} />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              ) : (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
                  Aucune donnée
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1 mt-2">
              {categoryData.map((item, idx) => (
                <div key={item.name} className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ background: COLORS[idx % COLORS.length] }} />
                    <span className="capitalize">{item.name}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">{item.value}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Card>
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Campaign Situation Table */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold mb-3">Situation des Campagnes</h2>
        <Card className="divide-y divide-border">
          {campaigns.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              Aucune campagne créée
            </div>
          ) : (
            campaigns.map((c) => {
              const statusLabels = {
                active: { label: 'Active', color: 'bg-accent/10 text-accent' },
                draft: { label: 'Brouillon', color: 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground' },
                paused: { label: 'En pause', color: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600' },
                completed: { label: 'Terminée', color: 'bg-primary/10 text-primary' },
                pending_review: { label: 'En révision', color: 'bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600' },
                rejected: { label: 'Rejetée', color: 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive' },
              };
              const status = statusLabels[c.status] || statusLabels.draft;
              const budgetSpent = c.budget_spent_dt || 0;
              const budgetTotal = c.budget_dt || 0;
              return (
                <div key={c.id} className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3">
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{c.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.brand_name}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-sm">
                    <div className="text-center">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Interactions</p>
                      <p className="font-semibold">{(c.current_interactions || 0).toLocaleString()}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-center hidden sm:block">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Budget</p>
                      <p className="font-semibold">{budgetSpent.toFixed(0)}/{budgetTotal.toFixed(0)} DT</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-center hidden md:block">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Complétion</p>
                      <p className="font-semibold">{c.completion_rate || 0}%</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-center hidden md:block">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Attention</p>
                      <p className="font-semibold">{c.attention_score || 0}/100</p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge className={status.color}>{status.label}</Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 106/123: /src/pages/entrepreneur/CampaignsPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { Plus, Megaphone, Eye, Coins, Trash2, Layers, Upload, Film } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';

const interactionCodeLabels = {
  qr_scan: '📱 QR Scan',
  video_streaming: '🎬 Vidéo Streaming',
  shoppable_click: '🛒 Shoppable Click',
  display_banner: '🖼️ Display Banner',
};

const PACK_PB = {
  eclat: 0.000349,
  expansion: 0.000174,
  prestige: 0.000116,
  absolu: 0.000087,
};

const campaignTypeLabels = {
  phygital: { label: 'Phygital', icon: '📺', badge: 'bg-primary/10 text-primary' },
  digital: { label: 'Digital', icon: '📱', badge: 'bg-accent/10 text-accent' },
};

const statusLabels = {
  active: 'Active', draft: 'Brouillon', paused: 'En pause',
  completed: 'Terminée', pending_review: 'En révision', rejected: 'Rejetée',
};

const statusColors = {
  active: 'bg-accent/10 text-accent',
  draft: 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground',
  paused: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600',
  completed: 'bg-primary/10 text-primary',
  pending_review: 'bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600',
  rejected: 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive',
};

export default function CampaignsPage() {
  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    title: '', brand_name: '', description: '', category: 'tech',
    interaction_category_id: '', campaign_type: 'phygital',
    budget_dt: '', video_url: '', video_duration_sec: 30,
    pool_type: 'both', qr_code_enabled: false, target_audience: '',
    start_date: '', end_date: '',
  });
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: interactionCategories = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['interaction-categories'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.InteractionCategory.filter({ status: 'active' }),
  });

  const selectedInteractionCat = interactionCategories.find(c => c.id === form.interaction_category_id);

  const { data: campaigns = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-campaigns'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Campaign.filter({ created_by_id: user?.id }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const createCampaign = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data) => {
      const budget = parseFloat(data.budget_dt) || 0;
      const cat = interactionCategories.find(c => c.id === data.interaction_category_id);
      const isDigital = data.campaign_type === 'digital';

      if (isDigital) {
        // Digital: 100% Pool B dégressif — pas de Pool A
        const pbCoeff = cat?.pb_coefficient || PACK_PB[cat?.code] || 0.000087;
        const firstGainB = budget * (pbCoeff / 100);
        return base44.entities.Campaign.create({
          ...data,
          budget_dt: budget,
          video_duration_sec: parseInt(data.video_duration_sec) || 30,
          campaign_type: 'digital',
          pool_type: 'pool_b',
          pool_a_gain_dt: 0,
          pool_b_gain_dt: parseFloat(firstGainB.toFixed(6)),
          gain_per_view_dt: parseFloat(firstGainB.toFixed(6)),
          qr_code_enabled: false,
          max_interactions: 0,
          status: 'pending_review',
        });
      }

      // Phygital: 40% Pool A (fixe 0,1 DT) + 60% Pool B (dégressif)
      const pbCoeffPhy = cat?.pb_coefficient || PACK_PB[cat?.code] || 0.000087;
      const poolBMassPhy = budget * 0.6;
      const firstGainBPhy = poolBMassPhy * (pbCoeffPhy / 100);
      return base44.entities.Campaign.create({
        ...data,
        budget_dt: budget,
        video_duration_sec: parseInt(data.video_duration_sec) || 30,
        campaign_type: 'phygital',
        gain_per_view_dt: parseFloat(firstGainBPhy.toFixed(6)),
        pool_a_gain_dt: cat?.price_per_interaction_dt || 0.1,
        pool_b_gain_dt: parseFloat(firstGainBPhy.toFixed(6)),
        max_interactions: 0,
        pool_type: 'both',
        status: 'pending_review',
      });
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-campaigns'] });
      setCreateOpen(false);
      setForm({ title: '', brand_name: '', description: '', category: 'tech', interaction_category_id: '', campaign_type: 'phygital', budget_dt: '', video_url: '', video_duration_sec: 30, pool_type: 'both', qr_code_enabled: false, target_audience: '', start_date: '', end_date: '' });
    },
  });

  const deleteCampaign = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id) => base44.entities.Campaign.delete(id),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-campaigns'] }),
  });

  const handleChange = (field, value) => setForm(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));

  const [videoUploading, setVideoUploading] = useState(false);

  const handleVideoUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.mp4')) {
      toast({ title: 'Format invalide', description: 'Le fichier doit être au format MP4', variant: 'destructive' });
      return;
    }
    setVideoUploading(true);
    try {
      const { file_url } = await base44.integrations.Core.UploadFile({ file });
      handleChange('video_url', file_url);
      handleChange('video_duration_sec', 10);
      toast({ title: 'Vidéo téléchargée', description: 'Format MP4 validé' });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur', description: 'Échec du téléchargement vidéo', variant: 'destructive' });
    }
    setVideoUploading(false);
  };

  const getPackPrice = (cat, type) => cat.total_mass_g_dt;

  const handleSelectCategory = (catId) => {
    const cat = interactionCategories.find(c => c.id === catId);
    const price = cat ? getPackPrice(cat, form.campaign_type) : null;
    setForm(prev => ({ ...prev, interaction_category_id: catId, budget_dt: price != null ? price.toString() : prev.budget_dt }));
  };

  const handleTypeChange = (type) => {
    const cat = interactionCategories.find(c => c.id === form.interaction_category_id);
    const price = cat ? getPackPrice(cat, type) : null;
    setForm(prev => ({ ...prev, campaign_type: type, budget_dt: price != null ? price.toString() : prev.budget_dt }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">Mes Campagnes</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">{campaigns.length} campagne(s)</p>
        </div>
        <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
          <DialogTrigger asChild>
            <Button className="gap-2"><Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Créer</Button>
          </DialogTrigger>
          <DialogContent className="max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <DialogHeader>
              <DialogTitle>Nouvelle Campagne</DialogTitle>
            </DialogHeader>
            <div className="space-y-4 mt-2">
              {/* Type de campagne */}
              <div>
                <Label className="mb-2 block">Type de Campagne</Label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleTypeChange('phygital')}
                    className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${form.campaign_type === 'phygital' ? 'border-primary bg-primary/10' : 'border-border hover:border-primary/40'}`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="text-lg">📺</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold">Phygital</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Écrans physiques + Web P-interact · 40% A / 60% B</p>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleTypeChange('digital')}
                    className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${form.campaign_type === 'digital' ? 'border-accent bg-accent/10' : 'border-border hover:border-accent/40'}`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="text-lg">📱</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold">Digital</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Web P-interact uniquement · 100% Pool B dégressif</p>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <Label>Titre</Label>
                  <Input value={form.title} onChange={e => handleChange('title', e.target.value)} className="mt-1" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label>Marque</Label>
                  <Input value={form.brand_name} onChange={e => handleChange('brand_name', e.target.value)} className="mt-1" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label>Description</Label>
                <Textarea value={form.description} onChange={e => handleChange('description', e.target.value)} className="mt-1" rows={3} />
              </div>
              {/* Catégorie d'interaction */}
              <div>
                <Label className="flex items-center gap-1"><Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Catégorie d'Interaction</Label>
                {interactionCategories.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-2 mt-1">
                    ⚠️ Aucune catégorie disponible — contactez l'administrateur pour en configurer.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-2">
                    {interactionCategories.map(cat => (
                      <button
                        key={cat.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleSelectCategory(cat.id)}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${
                          form.interaction_category_id === cat.id
                            ? 'border-primary bg-primary/10'
                            : 'border-border hover:border-primary/40'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span className="text-base">{cat.icon || '📱'}</span>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="text-xs font-semibold truncate">{cat.name}</p>
                          <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">{(cat.total_mass_g_dt || 0).toLocaleString()} DT</p>
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Aperçu répartition si catégorie sélectionnée */}
                {selectedInteractionCat && form.campaign_type === 'phygital' && (
                  <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-muted/50 border border-border">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground mb-2">Répartition Pool A / Pool B (40/60)</p>
                    <div className="h-2.5 rounded-full bg-muted overflow-hidden flex mb-2">
                      <div className="h-full bg-primary rounded-l-full" style={{ width: '40%' }} />
                      <div className="h-full bg-accent rounded-r-full" style={{ width: '60%' }} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-center">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Pool A (40%) — Fixe</p>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">0,1 DT/inter.</p>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Pool B (60%) — Dégressif</p>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-accent">{(selectedInteractionCat.pool_b_amount_dt || 0).toFixed(5)} DT</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {selectedInteractionCat && form.campaign_type === 'digital' && (
                  <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-accent/5 border border-accent/20">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-accent mb-2">100% Pool B — Dégressif (Digital)</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Budget G (100% Pool B)</span>
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-accent">{(selectedInteractionCat.total_mass_g_dt || 0).toLocaleString()} DT</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="h-2.5 rounded-full bg-muted overflow-hidden mb-2">
                      <div className="h-full bg-accent rounded-full" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground text-center">
                      100% du budget G en Pool B · Gain = Solde × PB
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div>
                <Label className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  Budget (DT)
                  {selectedInteractionCat && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground font-normal">
                      — Prix Pack {form.campaign_type === 'digital' ? 'Digital (100% Pool B)' : 'Phygital (40/60)'}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </Label>
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  value={form.budget_dt}
                  onChange={e => handleChange('budget_dt', e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1"
                  readOnly={!!selectedInteractionCat}
                  placeholder={selectedInteractionCat ? '' : 'Sélectionnez un pack'}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label className="flex items-center gap-1"><Film className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Support Vidéo (MP4 / 1920x1080 / 10s)</Label>
                <label className="cursor-pointer block">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-border hover:border-primary/40 transition-colors text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
                    {videoUploading ? 'Téléchargement...' : form.video_url ? 'Vidéo téléchargée ✓' : 'Télécharger un fichier MP4'}
                  </div>
                  <input type="file" accept="video/mp4" onChange={handleVideoUpload} className="hidden" />
                </label>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Format requis: MP4 · Résolution: 1920×1080 (Full HD) · Durée fixe: 10 secondes</p>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <Label>Date début</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={form.start_date} onChange={e => handleChange('start_date', e.target.value)} className="mt-1" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label>Date fin</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={form.end_date} onChange={e => handleChange('end_date', e.target.value)} className="mt-1" />
                </div>
              </div>
              {form.campaign_type === 'phygital' && (
                <div>
                  <Label>Pool de Distribution</Label>
                  <Select value={form.pool_type} onValueChange={v => handleChange('pool_type', v)}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="mt-1"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="pool_a">Pool A (Physique)</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="pool_b">Pool B (Digital)</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="both">Les Deux</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
              )}
              {form.campaign_type === 'phygital' && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <Label>QR Code Dynamique</Label>
                  <Switch checked={form.qr_code_enabled} onCheckedChange={v => handleChange('qr_code_enabled', v)} />
                </div>
              )}
              {form.campaign_type === 'digital' && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-accent/5 border border-accent/20 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  📱 <strong>Campagne Digital :</strong> Diffusion sur la plateforme P-interact uniquement.
                  100% du budget G va en <strong>Pool B</strong> (dégressif) — Gain = Solde × PB.
                  Pas de QR Code ni d'écrans physiques.
                </div>
              )}
              <Button onClick={() => createCampaign.mutate(form)} disabled={!form.title || !form.brand_name || !form.budget_dt} className="w-full">
                Soumettre pour Révision
              </Button>
            </div>
          </DialogContent>
        </Dialog>
      </div>

      {/* Campaigns List */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {isLoading ? (
          Array(3).fill(0).map((_, i) => (
            <Card key={i} className="p-4 animate-pulse">
              <div className="flex gap-4">
                <div className="w-24 h-16 bg-muted rounded-lg" />
                <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                  <div className="h-4 bg-muted rounded w-1/3" />
                  <div className="h-3 bg-muted rounded w-1/4" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))
        ) : campaigns.length === 0 ? (
          <Card className="p-12 text-center">
            <Megaphone className="w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground/30 mx-auto mb-3" />
            <p className="text-muted-foreground">Créez votre première campagne</p>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          campaigns.map((campaign, idx) => (
            <motion.div key={campaign.id} initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} transition={{ delay: idx * 0.05 }}>
              <Card className="p-4 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="w-20 h-14 rounded-lg bg-muted overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                    {campaign.thumbnail_url ? (
                      <img src={campaign.thumbnail_url} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="" />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/20 to-accent/20">
                        <Megaphone className="w-5 h-5 text-primary/40" />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{campaign.title}</p>
                      <Badge className={(campaignTypeLabels[campaign.campaign_type] || campaignTypeLabels.phygital).badge + ' text-[10px]'}>
                        {(campaignTypeLabels[campaign.campaign_type] || campaignTypeLabels.phygital).icon} {(campaignTypeLabels[campaign.campaign_type] || campaignTypeLabels.phygital).label}
                      </Badge>
                      <Badge className={statusColors[campaign.status] + ' text-[10px]'}>
                        {statusLabels[campaign.status]}
                      </Badge>
                      {campaign.workflow_stage > 0 && campaign.workflow_stage < 9 && (
                        <Badge className="bg-primary/10 text-primary text-[10px]">
                          Étape {campaign.workflow_stage}/9
                        </Badge>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{campaign.brand_name} • {campaign.category}</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mt-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Eye className="w-3 h-3" /> {(campaign.current_interactions || 0).toLocaleString()}</span>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Coins className="w-3 h-3" /> {(campaign.budget_spent_dt || 0).toFixed(0)}/{(campaign.budget_dt || 0).toFixed(0)} DT</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => deleteCampaign.mutate(campaign.id)} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive">
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </Card>
            </motion.div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER 107/123: /src/pages/entrepreneur/EntrepreneurDashboard.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import StatCard from '@/components/shared/StatCard';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Megaphone, Eye, BarChart3, Coins, ArrowRight, TrendingUp, Zap, Ticket } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { AreaChart, Area, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';
import { formatDT } from '@/lib/format';

const mockChartData = [
  { day: 'Lun', interactions: 245, earnings: 12.5 },
  { day: 'Mar', interactions: 380, earnings: 19.2 },
  { day: 'Mer', interactions: 310, earnings: 15.7 },
  { day: 'Jeu', interactions: 520, earnings: 26.1 },
  { day: 'Ven', interactions: 470, earnings: 23.8 },
  { day: 'Sam', interactions: 620, earnings: 31.2 },
  { day: 'Dim', interactions: 410, earnings: 20.8 },
];

export default function EntrepreneurDashboard() {
  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const isPending = user?.account_status === 'pending_validation';
  const displayName =
    [user?.first_name, user?.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ') ||
    user?.full_name || user?.company_name || 'Annonceur';

  const { data: campaigns = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-campaigns'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Campaign.filter({ created_by_id: user?.id }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const { data: returnedVouchers = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['returned-vouchers'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ entrepreneur_id: user?.id, status: 'returned' }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const returnedVouchersValue = returnedVouchers.reduce((s, v) => {
    const remaining = v.remaining_value_dt || (v.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
    return s + remaining - (v.wholesaler_deduction_dt || 0);
  }, 0);

  const activeCampaigns = campaigns.filter(c => c.status === 'active');
  const totalBudget = campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.budget_dt || 0), 0);
  const totalSpent = campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.budget_spent_dt || 0), 0);
  const totalInteractions = campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.current_interactions || 0), 0);
  const avgCompletion = campaigns.length > 0
    ? campaigns.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.completion_rate || 0), 0) / campaigns.length
    : 0;

  const statusColors = {
    active: 'bg-accent/10 text-accent',
    draft: 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground',
    paused: 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600',
    completed: 'bg-primary/10 text-primary',
    pending_review: 'bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600',
    rejected: 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive',
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">Bienvenue, {displayName}</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Nom du titulaire du compte : {displayName} — Pilotez vos campagnes et suivez la performance</p>
        </div>
        <Link to="/campaigns">
          <Button className="gap-2" disabled={isPending}>
            <Megaphone className="w-4 h-4" /> Nouvelle Campagne
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <StatCard title="Campagnes Actives" value={activeCampaigns.length} icon={Megaphone} variant="primary" />
        <StatCard title="Interactions" value={totalInteractions.toLocaleString()} icon={Eye} trend={24} trendLabel="7j" />
        <StatCard title="Budget Dépensé" value={`${formatDT(totalSpent)} DT`} subtitle={`sur ${formatDT(totalBudget)} DT`} icon={Coins} variant="accent" />
        <StatCard title="Taux Complétion" value={`${avgCompletion.toFixed(0)}%`} icon={BarChart3} variant="warning" trend={8} />
      </div>

      {/* Porte-feuille Bons Retournés */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.05 }}>
        <Card className="p-5 bg-gradient-to-br from-accent/5 to-primary/5 border-accent/20">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-accent/10">
                <Ticket className="w-6 h-6 text-accent" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Porte-feuille Bons Retournés</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-heading font-bold">{formatDT(returnedVouchersValue)} <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">DT</span></p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{returnedVouchers.length} bon(s) retourné(s) — circuit citoyen → commerçant → annonceur</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Link to="/campaigns">
              <Button className="gap-2" disabled={isPending || returnedVouchersValue < 25}>
                <Coins className="w-4 h-4" /> Payer une Campagne
              </Button>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Chart */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.1 }}>
        <Card className="p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" /> Performance Hebdomadaire
            </h2>
            <Badge variant="outline">7 derniers jours</Badge>
          </div>
          <div className="h-64">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <AreaChart data={mockChartData}>
                <defs>
                  <linearGradient id="interactionGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                    <stop offset="0%" stopColor="hsl(250, 80%, 60%)" stopOpacity={0.3} />
                    <stop offset="100%" stopColor="hsl(250, 80%, 60%)" stopOpacity={0} />
                  </linearGradient>
                </defs>
                <XAxis dataKey="day" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                <Tooltip
                  contentStyle={{
                    background: 'hsl(228, 25%, 11%)',
                    border: 'none',
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                    color: 'white',
                    fontSize: '12px',
                  }}
                />
                <Area type="monotone" dataKey="interactions" stroke="hsl(250, 80%, 60%)" fill="url(#interactionGrad)" strokeWidth={2} />
              </AreaChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Recent Campaigns */}
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold">Campagnes Récentes</h2>
          <Link to="/campaigns">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="gap-1 text-primary">
              Gérer <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
        <Card className="divide-y divide-border">
          {campaigns.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
              <Megaphone className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground/30" />
              <p className="text-sm">Aucune campagne créée</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            campaigns.slice(0, 5).map((campaign) => (
              <div key={campaign.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Megaphone className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{campaign.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{campaign.brand_name} • {(campaign.current_interactions || 0).toLocaleString()} interactions</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="text-right hidden sm:block">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{formatDT(campaign.budget_spent_dt)} / {formatDT(campaign.budget_dt)} DT</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Complétion: {campaign.completion_rate || 0}%</p>
                  </div>
                  <Badge className={statusColors[campaign.status]}>
                    {campaign.status === 'active' ? 'Active' : campaign.status === 'draft' ? 'Brouillon' : campaign.status === 'paused' ? 'En pause' : campaign.status}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER 108/123: /src/pages/entrepreneur/QRCodesPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { QrCode, ExternalLink, Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export default function QRCodesPage() {
  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: campaigns = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-campaigns-qr'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Campaign.filter({ created_by_id: user?.id, qr_code_enabled: true }),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">QR Codes Dynamiques</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Gérez les codes pour vos écrans physiques (Pool A)</p>
      </div>

      {campaigns.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-12 text-center">
          <QrCode className="w-12 h-12 text-muted-foreground/30 mx-auto mb-3" />
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Aucune campagne avec QR code activé</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">Activez les QR codes dans les paramètres de campagne</p>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {campaigns.map((campaign) => {
            const qrUrl = `${window.location.origin}/watch?campaign=${campaign.id}&pool=a`;
            return (
              <Card key={campaign.id} className="p-5 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                  <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-xl bg-white border-2 border-border flex items-center justify-center p-2">
                    <QrCode className="w-full h-full text-foreground" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-sm">{campaign.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{campaign.brand_name}</p>
                    <Badge className="mt-2 bg-primary/10 text-primary text-[10px]">Pool A</Badge>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        size="sm"
                        className="text-xs gap-1"
                        onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(qrUrl); toast.success('URL copiée'); }}
                      >
                        <Copy className="w-3 h-3" /> Copier
                      </Button>
                      <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="text-xs gap-1" asChild>
                        <a href={qrUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                          <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" /> Ouvrir
                        </a>
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER 109/123: /src/pages/entrepreneur/VouchersPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Gift, Plus, Check, Coins, Eye, CheckCircle2, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

const VOUCHER_VALUES = [25, 50, 100, 200];

const genCode = () => 'BC-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8).toUpperCase();

export default function VouchersPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  // "issued" vouchers with no citizen = configured values (templates)
  const { data: configs = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-voucher-configs'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ entrepreneur_id: user?.id, status: 'issued' }, '-created_date', 100),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  // Extracted/used vouchers by citizens
  const { data: extracted = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['my-vouchers-extracted'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ entrepreneur_id: user?.id }, '-created_date', 200),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const configuredValues = [...new Set(configs.map((v) => v.value_dt))];
  const extractedVouchers = extracted.filter((v) => v.status === 'extracted' || v.status === 'used');
  const usedVouchers = extracted.filter((v) => v.status === 'used');
  const totalDistributed = extractedVouchers.reduce((s, v) => s + (v.value_dt || 0), 0);

  const toggleValue = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (value) => {
      const existing = configs.find((v) => v.value_dt === value);
      if (existing) {
        return base44.entities.Voucher.delete(existing.id);
      }
      return base44.entities.Voucher.create({
        title: `Bon Cadeau ${value} DT`,
        code: genCode(),
        entrepreneur_id: user.id,
        advertiser_name: user.company_name || user.email,
        value_dt: value,
        status: 'issued',
      });
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-voucher-configs'] });
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur', description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });
    },
  });

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <Gift className="w-6 h-6 text-primary" /> Bons Cadeaux
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">
          Configurez les valeurs de vos Bons Cadeaux. Quand un citoyen atteint la valeur, il extrait son Bon Cadeau pour acheter vos produits.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Value configuration */}
      <Card className="p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
          <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold">Valeurs disponibles</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-4">
          Activez les valeurs que les citoyens pourront extraire. Aucune quantité limite, aucune date d'expiration.
        </p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          {VOUCHER_VALUES.map((value, idx) => {
            const active = configuredValues.includes(value);
            return (
              <motion.div
                key={value}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: idx * 0.05 }}
              >
                <Card
                  className={`p-5 cursor-pointer transition-all hover:shadow-lg ${
                    active ? 'border-accent bg-accent/5 ring-2 ring-accent/30' : 'border-border hover:border-primary/30'
                  }`}
                  onClick={() => toggleValue.mutate(value)}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <Gift className={`w-5 h-5 ${active ? 'text-accent' : 'text-muted-foreground/40'}`} />
                    {active ? (
                      <Badge className="bg-accent/10 text-accent text-[10px] gap-1">
                        <Check className="w-3 h-3" /> Activé
                      </Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px]">Désactivé</Badge>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{value} DT</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                    {active ? 'Bon Cadeau disponible' : 'Cliquer pour activer'}
                  </p>
                </Card>
              </motion.div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Gift className="w-5 h-5 text-primary mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{configuredValues.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Valeurs configurées</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Eye className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{extractedVouchers.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Bons extraits</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-accent mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{usedVouchers.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Bons utilisés</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Coins className="w-5 h-5 text-primary mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{totalDistributed.toFixed(0)}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Valeur distribuée (DT)</p>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Extracted vouchers list */}
      <Card>
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Gift className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" /> Bons Cadeaux extraits par les citoyens
          </h2>
        </div>
        {extractedVouchers.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            <Gift className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground/30" />
            Aucun Bon Cadeau extrait pour le moment
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-border">
            {extractedVouchers.slice(0, 30).map((v) => (
              <div key={v.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Gift className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{v.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">{v.code}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{(v.value_dt || 0).toFixed(0)} DT</p>
                  <Badge className={v.status === 'used' ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent text-[10px]' : 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 text-[10px]'}>
                    {v.status === 'used' ? 'Utilisé' : 'Extrait'}
                  </Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER 110/123: /src/pages/ForgotPassword.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Mail, ArrowLeft, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPasswordRequest(email);
    } catch {
      // Always show success regardless
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setSent(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Mail}
      title="Réinitialiser le mot de passe"
      subtitle="Nous vous enverrons un lien de réinitialisation"
      footer={
        <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1" />Retour à la connexion
        </Link>
      }
    >
      {sent ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          Si un compte existe avec cet email, vous recevrez un lien de réinitialisation sous peu.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="email">Adresse email</Label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
              <Input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                autoFocus
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="pl-10 h-12"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                Envoi...
                </>
                ) : (
                "Envoyer le lien"
            )}
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}

FICHIER 111/123: /src/pages/Login.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { LogIn, Mail, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import PasswordInput from "@/components/ui/password-input";
import GoogleIcon from "@/components/GoogleIcon";

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.loginViaEmailPassword(email, password);
      window.location.href = "/";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Email ou mot de passe invalide");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGoogle = () => {
    base44.auth.loginWithProvider("google", "/");
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={LogIn}
      title="Bon retour"
      subtitle="Connectez-vous à votre compte"
      footer={
        <div className="space-y-2 text-center">
          <div>
            Pas encore de compte ?{" "}
            <Link to="/register" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
              Créer un compte
            </Link>
          </div>
          <div>
            <Link to="/welcome" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary hover:underline">
              ← Page d'accueil P-interact
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        className="w-full h-12 text-sm font-medium mb-6"
        onClick={handleGoogle}
      >
        <GoogleIcon className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
        Continuer avec Google
      </Button>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full border-t border-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase">
          <span className="bg-card px-3 text-muted-foreground">ou</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">Adresse email</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Mot de passe</Label>
            <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline">
              Mot de passe oublié ?
            </Link>
          </div>
          <PasswordInput
            id="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            placeholder="••••••••"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Connexion...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Se connecter"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
