FICHIER 112/123: /src/pages/merchant/AffiliationPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { UserPlus, Copy, CheckCircle2, QrCode, Users, Link2 } from 'lucide-react';
import QRCodeCard from '@/components/shared/QRCodeCard';

export default function AffiliationPage() {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [copiedQr, setCopiedQr] = useState(false);

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: affiliatedCitizens = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['affiliated-citizens', user?.id],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.User.filter({ affiliated_by: user?.id }),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const code = user?.affiliation_code || '—';
  const affiliationLink = `${window.location.origin}/register?ref=${code}`;

  const copyLink = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(affiliationLink);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const copyCode = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
    setCopiedQr(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedQr(false), 2000);
  };

  if (!user?.affiliation_code) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
        <Card className="p-8 text-center space-y-3">
          <UserPlus className="w-10 h-10 text-muted-foreground/30 mx-auto" />
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Votre code d'affiliation sera généré une fois votre compte validé par l'administrateur.
          </p>
        </Card>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <UserPlus className="w-6 h-6 text-teal-600" /> Affiliation de Citoyens
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">
          Partagez votre code ou lien pour affilier des citoyens à votre commerce
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Code card + QR code */}
      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <Card className="p-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-500/5 to-accent/5 border-teal-500/20">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-teal-700 mb-1">Votre Code d'Affiliation</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-4">
            Donnez ce code aux citoyens lors de leur inscription, ou partagez le lien direct.
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap mb-4">
            <code className="text-3xl font-mono font-black tracking-[0.2em] bg-card px-5 py-3 rounded-xl border border-teal-500/20">
              {code}
            </code>
            <Button onClick={copyCode} variant="outline" className="gap-1.5">
              {copiedQr ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-accent" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
              {copiedQr ? 'Copié !' : 'Copier le code'}
            </Button>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 bg-card rounded-lg border border-border">
            <Link2 className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate flex-1">{affiliationLink}</span>
            <Button onClick={copyLink} size="sm" variant="ghost" className="gap-1 h-7 flex-shrink-0">
              {copied ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-accent" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              {copied ? 'Copié' : 'Copier le lien'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/5 to-card border-primary/20">
          <QRCodeCard
            value={affiliationLink}
            size={200}
            title="QR Code d'Affiliation"
            subtitle="Le citoyen scanne ce QR code pour s'inscrire et être affilié à votre commerce"
            fileName={`qr-affiliation-${code}`}
          />
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Users className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600 mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{affiliatedCitizens.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Citoyens affiliés</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <QrCode className="w-5 h-5 text-primary mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">5%</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Commission gains citoyens</p>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* How it works */}
      <Card className="p-5">
        <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" /> Comment ça marche
        </h2>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {[
            { n: 1, t: 'Partagez votre code ou lien', d: 'Donnez votre code d\'affiliation au citoyen ou envoyez-lui le lien.' },
            { n: 2, t: 'Le citoyen s\'inscrit', d: 'Il crée son compte Citoyen avec votre code — vous êtes automatiquement lié.' },
            { n: 3, t: 'Il interagit avec les campagnes', d: 'À chaque interaction gagnante, vous recevez 5% de commission.' },
            { n: 4, t: 'Gains sur bons d\'achat', d: 'Vous recevez aussi 10% sur chaque bon d\'achat utilisé dans votre commerce.' },
          ].map((s) => (
            <div key={s.n} className="flex gap-3">
              <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-700 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">{s.n}</div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{s.t}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{s.d}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Affiliated citizens list */}
      <Card>
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" /> Citoyens affiliés ({affiliatedCitizens.length})
          </h2>
        </div>
        {affiliatedCitizens.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            <Users className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground/30" />
            Partagez votre code pour commencer à affilier des citoyens.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-border">
            {affiliatedCitizens.map((c) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="p-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-teal-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-teal-600">
                      {(c.first_name?.[0] || c.email?.[0] || '?').toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">
                      {[c.first_name, c.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || c.email}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.email}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Badge className={c.account_status === 'active' ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent text-[10px]' : 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground text-[10px]'}>
                  {c.account_status === 'active' ? 'Actif' : c.account_status}
                </Badge>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 113/123: /src/pages/merchant/MerchantDashboard.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Store, CheckCircle2, Clock, ScanLine, Coins, Ticket, Camera, Wallet, Send, ArrowRight, Building2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';
import VoucherScanner from '@/components/shared/VoucherScanner';
import { formatDT } from '@/lib/format';

export default function MerchantDashboard() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [scannerOpen, setScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [paymentOpen, setPaymentOpen] = useState(false);
  const [paymentAmount, setPaymentAmount] = useState('');
  const [paymentRecipient, setPaymentRecipient] = useState('');

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const isPending = user?.account_status === 'pending_validation';
  const displayName =
    [user?.first_name, user?.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ') ||
    user?.full_name || 'Commerçant';

  const { data: wallet } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['merchant-wallet'],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const wallets = await base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: user?.id });
      return wallets[0] || null;
    },
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const { data: validatedVouchers = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['merchant-vouchers'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ merchant_id: user?.id }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const validateVoucher = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (voucherCode) => {
      const vouchers = await base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ code: voucherCode, status: 'extracted' });
      if (vouchers.length === 0) throw new Error('Bon introuvable ou non extractible');
      const voucher = vouchers[0];
      const remaining = voucher.real_value_dt || (voucher.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
      const commission = (voucher.value_dt || 0) * 0.10;
      // Update voucher
      await base44.entities.Voucher.update(voucher.id, {
        status: 'used',
        merchant_id: user.id,
        remaining_value_dt: remaining,
      });
      // Credit merchant wallet
      const wallets = await base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: user.id });
      if (wallets.length > 0) {
        const w = wallets[0];
        await base44.entities.Wallet.update(w.id, {
          balance_dt: (w.balance_dt || 0) + commission,
          voucher_commission_dt: (w.voucher_commission_dt || 0) + commission,
          total_earned_dt: (w.total_earned_dt || 0) + commission,
        });
      } else {
        await base44.entities.Wallet.create({
          user_id: user.id,
          balance_dt: commission,
          voucher_commission_dt: commission,
          total_earned_dt: commission,
        });
      }
      return { voucher, commission, remaining };
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['merchant-vouchers'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['merchant-wallet'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['merchant-used-vouchers'] });
      toast({
        title: 'Bon Cadeau validé',
        description: `Commission 10%: ${formatDT(data.commission)} DT — Reste ${formatDT(data.remaining)} DT en bons cumulés`,
      });
      setCode('');
      setScannerOpen(false);
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur', description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });
    },
  });

  const processPayment = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const amount = parseFloat(paymentAmount);
      if (!amount || amount <= 0) throw new Error('Montant invalide');
      if (!wallet || (wallet.balance_dt || 0) < amount) throw new Error('Solde insuffisant');
      await base44.entities.Wallet.update(wallet.id, {
        balance_dt: (wallet.balance_dt || 0) - amount,
        total_withdrawn_dt: (wallet.total_withdrawn_dt || 0) + amount,
      });
      return amount;
    },
    onSuccess: (amount) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['merchant-wallet'] });
      toast({ title: 'Paiement effectué', description: `${formatDT(amount)} DT transférés à ${paymentRecipient || 'bénéficiaire'}` });
      setPaymentOpen(false);
      setPaymentAmount('');
      setPaymentRecipient('');
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur', description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });
    },
  });

  const handleValidate = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!code.trim()) return;
    validateVoucher.mutate(code.trim().toUpperCase());
  };

  const handleScan = (scannedCode) => {
    validateVoucher.mutate(scannedCode.toUpperCase());
  };

  const walletBalance = wallet?.balance_dt || 0;
  const affiliationEarnings = wallet?.affiliation_earnings_dt || 0;
  const voucherCommission = wallet?.voucher_commission_dt || 0;

  const totalValidated = validatedVouchers.length;
  const totalValue = validatedVouchers.reduce((s, v) => s + (v.value_dt || 0), 0);
  const usedVouchers = validatedVouchers.filter((v) => v.status === 'used');

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <Store className="w-6 h-6 text-teal-600" /> Bienvenue, {displayName}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Nom du titulaire du compte : {displayName} — Acceptez les Bons Cadeaux et gérez vos gains</p>
      </div>

      {/* Profile */}
      <Card className="p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-6 flex-wrap">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-teal-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-teal-600">
              {(user?.first_name?.[0] || user?.email?.[0] || '?').toUpperCase()}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold text-lg truncate">{displayName}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{user?.email}</p>
          </div>
          {isPending ? (
            <Badge className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 gap-1">
              <Clock className="w-3 h-3" /> En attente
            </Badge>
          ) : (
            <Badge className="bg-accent/10 text-accent gap-1">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Validé
            </Badge>
          )}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Merchant Gains Wallet */}
      <Card className="p-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-500/5 to-accent/5 border-teal-500/20">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-teal-500/10">
              <Wallet className="w-6 h-6 text-teal-600" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Porte-feuille Gains</p>
              <p className="text-3xl font-heading font-bold">{formatDT(walletBalance)} <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">DT</span></p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Button onClick={() => setPaymentOpen(true)} className="gap-2" disabled={walletBalance <= 0}>
            <Send className="w-4 h-4" /> Paiement
          </Button>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-card/60 border border-border">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Affiliation Brut (5%)</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold mt-1 text-primary">{formatDT(affiliationEarnings)} DT</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-card/60 border border-border">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Commission Bons (10%)</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold mt-1 text-accent">{formatDT(voucherCommission)} DT</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Voucher Validation */}
      <Card className="p-6">
        <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <ScanLine className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" /> Validation des Bons Cadeaux
        </h2>
        <Button size="lg" onClick={() => setScannerOpen(true)} className="w-full gap-2 h-16 text-base">
          <Camera className="w-6 h-6" /> Scanner le QR Code
        </Button>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 my-4">
          <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-border" />
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">ou saisie manuelle</span>
          <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-border" />
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleValidate} className="flex gap-2">
          <Input
            value={code}
            onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Code du bon (ex: BC-XXXXXX)"
            className="font-mono"
          />
          <Button type="submit" variant="outline" disabled={validateVoucher.isPending} className="gap-1.5">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Valider
          </Button>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <VoucherScanner open={scannerOpen} onOpenChange={setScannerOpen} onScan={handleScan} />

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Ticket className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600 mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{totalValidated}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Bons validés</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Coins className="w-5 h-5 text-primary mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{formatDT(totalValue)}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Valeur totale (DT)</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-4">
          <Coins className="w-5 h-5 text-accent mb-2" />
          <p className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">{formatDT(voucherCommission)}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Commission (DT)</p>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Link to Bons d'achats cumulés */}
      {usedVouchers.length > 0 && (
        <Link to="/merchant/vouchers">
          <Card className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-teal-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Bons d'Achats Cumulés</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{usedVouchers.length} bon(s) accepté(s) — faites vos achats en gros</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" />
          </Card>
        </Link>
      )}

      {/* Validated Vouchers List */}
      <Card>
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" /> Bons Validés
          </h2>
        </div>
        {validatedVouchers.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            <Ticket className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground/30" />
            Aucun bon validé pour le moment
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-border">
            {validatedVouchers.map((v) => {
              const remaining = v.remaining_value_dt || v.real_value_dt || (v.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
              return (
                <div key={v.id} className="p-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{v.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">{v.code}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{formatDT(v.value_dt)} DT</p>
                    {v.status === 'used' && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-accent">Reste: {formatDT(remaining)} DT</p>
                    )}
                    <Badge className="bg-accent/10 text-accent text-[10px]">
                      {v.status === 'used' ? 'Validé' : v.status}
                    </Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Payment Dialog */}
      <Dialog open={paymentOpen} onOpenChange={setPaymentOpen}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Send className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" /> Paiement
            </DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-4 mt-2">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 border border-border">
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Solde disponible</p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold">{formatDT(walletBalance)} DT</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-1">Bénéficiaire</p>
              <Input
                value={paymentRecipient}
                onChange={(e) => setPaymentRecipient(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Nom du bénéficiaire (annonceur / grossiste)"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-1">Montant (DT)</p>
              <Input
                type="number"
                value={paymentAmount}
                onChange={(e) => setPaymentAmount(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="0.00"
                max={walletBalance}
              />
            </div>
            <Button
              className="w-full gap-2"
              onClick={() => processPayment.mutate()}
              disabled={processPayment.isPending || !paymentAmount || !paymentRecipient}
            >
              {processPayment.isPending ? 'Traitement...' : `Transférer ${paymentAmount || '0'} DT`}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 114/123: /src/pages/merchant/MerchantVouchersPage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Ticket, Building2, QrCode, Send, Coins } from 'lucide-react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import QRCodeCard from '@/components/shared/QRCodeCard';
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';

export default function MerchantVouchersPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [transferVoucher, setTransferVoucher] = useState(null);
  const [transferType, setTransferType] = useState('direct');

  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const { data: usedVouchers = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['merchant-used-vouchers'],
    queryFn: () => base44.entities.Voucher.filter({ merchant_id: user?.id, status: 'used' }, '-created_date'),
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  });

  const transferVoucherMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ voucher, type }) => {
      const remaining = voucher.remaining_value_dt || voucher.real_value_dt || (voucher.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
      let entrepreneurAmount = remaining;
      let wholesalerDeduction = 0;
      if (type === 'wholesaler') {
        wholesalerDeduction = remaining * 0.1;
        entrepreneurAmount = remaining * 0.9;
      }
      await base44.entities.Voucher.update(voucher.id, {
        status: 'returned',
        returned_to_entrepreneur: true,
        transfer_type: type,
        wholesaler_deduction_dt: wholesalerDeduction,
      });
      const entrepreneurWallets = await base44.entities.Wallet.filter({ user_id: voucher.entrepreneur_id });
      if (entrepreneurWallets.length > 0) {
        const w = entrepreneurWallets[0];
        await base44.entities.Wallet.update(w.id, {
          returned_vouchers_value_dt: (w.returned_vouchers_value_dt || 0) + entrepreneurAmount,
        });
      } else {
        await base44.entities.Wallet.create({
          user_id: voucher.entrepreneur_id,
          returned_vouchers_value_dt: entrepreneurAmount,
        });
      }
      return { entrepreneurAmount, wholesalerDeduction };
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['merchant-used-vouchers'] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['merchant-wallet'] });
      toast({
        title: 'Bon transféré',
        description: `${data.entrepreneurAmount.toFixed(2)} DT transférés à l'annonceur${data.wholesalerDeduction > 0 ? ` (grossiste: ${data.wholesalerDeduction.toFixed(2)} DT)` : ''}`,
      });
      setTransferVoucher(null);
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      toast({ title: 'Erreur', description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });
    },
  });

  // Group by advertiser
  const advertiserMap = {};
  usedVouchers.forEach((v) => {
    const advId = v.entrepreneur_id;
    if (!advId) return;
    if (!advertiserMap[advId]) {
      advertiserMap[advId] = { id: advId, name: v.advertiser_name || 'Annonceur', vouchers: [], totalRemaining: 0 };
    }
    advertiserMap[advId].vouchers.push(v);
    advertiserMap[advId].totalRemaining += v.remaining_value_dt || v.real_value_dt || (v.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
  });
  const segments = Object.values(advertiserMap);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <Ticket className="w-6 h-6 text-teal-600" /> Bons d'Achats Cumulés
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">Bons acceptés segmentés par annonceur — faites vos achats en gros</p>
      </div>

      {segments.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">
          <Ticket className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 text-muted-foreground/30" />
          <p className="text-sm">Aucun bon cumulé. Acceptez des Bons Cadeaux pour les voir ici.</p>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        segments.map((seg) => (
          <div key={seg.id}>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-heading font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
                <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" /> {seg.name}
              </h2>
              <Badge className="bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-600">
                <Coins className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" /> {seg.totalRemaining.toFixed(2)} DT cumulés
              </Badge>
            </div>
            <Card className="divide-y divide-border">
              {seg.vouchers.map((v) => {
                const remaining = v.remaining_value_dt || v.real_value_dt || (v.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
                return (
                  <div key={v.id} className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-teal-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <Ticket className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium">{v.title}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">{v.code}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="text-right mr-2">
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold">{remaining.toFixed(2)} DT</p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Valeur restante</p>
                      </div>
                      <Dialog>
                        <DialogTrigger asChild>
                          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1 h-8">
                            <QrCode className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Afficher Bon
                          </Button>
                        </DialogTrigger>
                        <DialogContent className="max-w-sm">
                          <DialogHeader>
                            <DialogTitle className="text-sm">Bon — {v.code}</DialogTitle>
                          </DialogHeader>
                          <QRCodeCard
                            value={v.code}
                            size={200}
                            title="Bon d'Achat Cumulé"
                            subtitle={`Valeur restante: ${remaining.toFixed(2)} DT — Scannez avec le grossiste`}
                            fileName={`bon-${v.code}`}
                          />
                        </DialogContent>
                      </Dialog>
                      <Button size="sm" className="gap-1 h-8" onClick={() => setTransferVoucher(v)}>
                        <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Transférer
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </Card>
          </div>
        ))
      )}

      {/* Transfer Dialog */}
      <Dialog open={!!transferVoucher} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setTransferVoucher(null)}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Send className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" /> Transférer le Bon
            </DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          {transferVoucher && (() => {
            const remaining = transferVoucher.remaining_value_dt || transferVoucher.real_value_dt || (transferVoucher.value_dt || 0) * 0.9;
            const wholesalerAmount = remaining * 0.9;
            return (
              <div className="space-y-4 mt-2">
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 border border-border">
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Bon</p>
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{transferVoucher.title} — {transferVoucher.code}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">Valeur restante: {remaining.toFixed(2)} DT</p>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setTransferType('direct')}
                    className={`p-4 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${transferType === 'direct' ? 'border-teal-500 bg-teal-500/10' : 'border-border'}`}
                  >
                    <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600 mb-2" />
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Annonceur direct</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{remaining.toFixed(2)} DT transférés</p>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setTransferType('wholesaler')}
                    className={`p-4 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${transferType === 'wholesaler' ? 'border-violet-500 bg-violet-500/10' : 'border-border'}`}
                  >
                    <Send className="w-5 h-5 text-violet-600 mb-2" />
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Grossiste</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{wholesalerAmount.toFixed(2)} DT (−10% grossiste)</p>
                  </button>
                </div>
                <Button
                  className="w-full gap-2"
                  onClick={() => transferVoucherMutation.mutate({ voucher: transferVoucher, type: transferType })}
                  disabled={transferVoucherMutation.isPending}
                >
                  {transferVoucherMutation.isPending
                    ? 'Transfert en cours...'
                    : `Transférer ${(transferType === 'direct' ? remaining : wholesalerAmount).toFixed(2)} DT`}
                </Button>
              </div>
            );
          })()}
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 115/123: /src/pages/OAuthConsent.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { appParams } from "@/lib/app-params";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

// App-side OAuth consent page for the app's MCP server. The platform redirects
// AI clients here (see base44/mcp/config.json `consent_path`) with an opaque
// `ctx` handle — the authorization request itself lives on the server. This page
// gates on the app-user session, fetches the display info for that handle, shows
// the categories of access being granted, and posts the approve/deny decision.
// Do not change the fetch calls, headers, or the `ctx` handle handling — styling
// and copy are safe to edit.
export default function OAuthConsent() {
  const ctx = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("ctx");
  const [info, setInfo] = useState(null);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [decided, setDecided] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [reconnect, setReconnect] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      let redirecting = false;
      try {
        if (!ctx) {
          setError("This authorization link is invalid or has expired.");
          return;
        }
        // Resolve the handle first: a dead handle must never render
        // approve/deny, and the response carries the app's configured login
        // route for the signed-out redirect below. Send the session (cookie +
        // bearer token) so the server can list the granted tools for a
        // signed-in user — the same auth the approve/deny call sends; without
        // it the display request is anonymous and shows no tools.
        const infoHeaders = {};
        if (appParams.token) infoHeaders.Authorization = "Bearer " + appParams.token;
        const res = await fetch(
          `/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/consent-info?handle=${encodeURIComponent(ctx)}`,
          { credentials: "include", headers: infoHeaders },
        );
        if (!res.ok) {
          setError("This authorization link is invalid or has expired.");
          return;
        }
        const data = await res.json();
        // Gate on the server's auth result, NOT base44.auth.isAuthenticated():
        // the SDK check runs the bearer path, so a cookie-only session (platform
        // login/SSO, or a private app with a stale localStorage token) would read
        // as signed-out and redirect — even though /consent-info just
        // authenticated this same request via its cookie fallback. data.authenticated
        // keeps the redirect decision in agreement with what the server returned.
        if (!data.authenticated) {
          // The short handle rides back in returnTo; login_path is
          // owner-configured and validated server-side as a same-origin path.
          // Send from_url too: a custom-auth app coerced to platform auth (e.g.
          // public_without_login under workspace SSO) serves the platform login,
          // which honors from_url rather than returnTo. Rebuild the query from
          // `ctx` alone — never forward window.location.search raw: the platform
          // resume returns from_url verbatim, so crafted extras on the consent
          // link (app_base_url, access_token, …) would ride through the login
          // round-trip and app-params.js would persist them into the freshly
          // authenticated session.
          const returnTo =
            window.location.pathname + "?ctx=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);
          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);
          redirecting = true; // keep the spinner while the browser navigates
          window.location.href =
            (data.login_path || "/login") + "?returnTo=" + encoded + "&from_url=" + encoded;
          return;
        }
        setInfo(data);
      } catch (e) {
        setError("Could not load this authorization request. Please try again.");
      } finally {
        if (!redirecting) setChecking(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [ctx]);

  const respond = async (action) => {
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
      // Cookie-backed sessions carry no token; sending "Bearer null" would
      // shadow the valid cookie, so add the header only when a token exists.
      if (appParams.token) headers.Authorization = "Bearer " + appParams.token;
      const res = await fetch(`/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/authorize-grant`, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({ ctx, action }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        // 401 = the session expired before the (single-use, still-unconsumed)
        // handle was spent; retrying the same controls re-sends the dead session
        // forever. Send the user back through login preserving `ctx` — the same
        // redirect the initial signed-out path uses — so they can return and
        // approve the still-valid handle.
        if (res.status === 401) {
          const returnTo = window.location.pathname + "?ctx=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);
          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);
          window.location.href =
            ((info && info.login_path) || "/login") + "?returnTo=" + encoded + "&from_url=" + encoded;
          return;
        }
        // These all come AFTER the single-use handle is atomically consumed
        // (409 tool set changed; 403 host/resource/app mismatch; 404 access
        // gone; 400 malformed/handle already used), so retrying can only 404.
        // Show a terminal reconnect state, not an impossible "try again".
        if ([400, 403, 404, 409].includes(res.status)) {
          let detail = "";
          try { detail = (await res.json()).detail; } catch (_) { /* keep default */ }
          setReconnect(detail || "This authorization can no longer be completed. Reconnect from your AI client to try again.");
          setSubmitting(false);
          return;
        }
        throw new Error("Could not complete authorization. Please try again.");
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      window.location.href = data.redirect_url;
      if (!/^https?:/i.test(data.redirect_url)) {
        // Custom-scheme redirect (native AI clients, e.g. cursor://): browsers
        // may block or not visibly navigate, so show a terminal state instead
        // of an eternal spinner.
        setDecided(action);
        setSubmitting(false);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message);
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (checking) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Authorize access">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-6 text-muted-foreground">
          <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
          Loading…
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const client = (info && info.client_name) || "An AI client";
  const appName = (info && info.app_name) || "this app";

  if (decided) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={ShieldCheck}
        title={decided === "approve" ? "Access granted" : "Access denied"}
        subtitle={`You can return to ${client} and close this window.`}
      />
    );
  }

  // Terminal: the authorization request is no longer valid (tool set changed +
  // handle consumed). Retrying can't succeed, so show reconnect guidance with
  // no approve/deny controls.
  if (reconnect) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Reconnect required">
        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {reconnect}
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  // No consent details means nothing trustworthy to approve: a failed
  // consent-info load (expired handle, rate limit, transient error) renders
  // the error alone, never the approve/deny controls.
  if (error && !info) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Authorize access">
        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const tools = Array.isArray(info.tools) ? info.tools : [];

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={ShieldCheck}
      title="Authorize access"
      subtitle={`${client} wants to access ${appName} on your behalf`}
    >
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-2">
        {tools.length ? `It will be able to use these tools in ${appName}:` : "No tools requested"}
      </p>
      {tools.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="space-y-2 text-sm mb-6">
          {tools.map((tool) => (
            <li key={tool.name} className="flex flex-col">
              <span className="text-foreground font-medium">
                {tool.title || tool.name}
              </span>
              {tool.description && (
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">{tool.description}</span>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          className="flex-1 h-12 font-medium"
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={() => respond("deny")}
        >
          Deny
        </Button>
        <Button
          className="flex-1 h-12 font-medium"
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={() => respond("approve")}
        >
          {submitting ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : null}
          Approve
        </Button>
      </div>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}

FICHIER 116/123: /src/pages/PendingValidation.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Clock, LogOut, BookOpen, CheckCircle2, Mail } from 'lucide-react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { ROLE_GUIDES } from '@/components/shared/RoleOnboarding';

const ROLE_LABELS = {
  entrepreneur: 'Annonceur',
  merchant: 'Commerçant',
};

export default function PendingValidation({ role, user }) {
  const guide = ROLE_GUIDES[role];
  const roleLabel = ROLE_LABELS[role] || role;
  const displayName = [user?.first_name, user?.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || user?.email || '';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center p-4 py-8">
      <div className="max-w-2xl w-full space-y-5">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="text-center space-y-3">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
            <Clock className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-600" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold">Compte en attente de validation</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">
              Bienvenue{displayName ? ` ${displayName}` : ''} ! Votre inscription en tant que <strong>{roleLabel}</strong> est en cours de vérification.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Status card */}
        <Card className="p-5 bg-amber-500/5 border-amber-500/20">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <Mail className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">Prochaine étape : Validation administrateur</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                L'équipe P-interact va vérifier votre demande. Vous recevrez un email de confirmation à{' '}
                <strong>{user?.email}</strong> dès que votre compte sera validé. L'accès complet à votre espace sera activé à ce moment.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* Instructions guide */}
        {guide && (
          <Card className={`p-5 ${guide.bg} ${guide.border}`}>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
              <BookOpen className={`w-5 h-5 ${guide.color}`} />
              <h2 className="font-heading font-bold text-sm">Guide de votre Espace {roleLabel}</h2>
            </div>
            <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">
              {guide.features.map((f, i) => {
                const Icon = f.icon;
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-card border border-border">
                    <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${guide.color} mb-1.5`} />
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold">{f.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{f.desc}</p>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground mb-2">Instructions du programme P-interact :</p>
              {guide.steps.map((step, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <span className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-card ${guide.color} flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px] flex-shrink-0`}>
                    {i + 1}
                  </span>
                  {step}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* What happens next */}
        <Card className="p-5">
          <h2 className="font-heading font-bold text-sm mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-accent" /> Ce qui se passe ensuite
          </h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[
              "L'administrateur examine votre demande d'inscription",
              `Vous recevez un email de confirmation à ${user?.email || 'votre adresse'}`,
              `Votre espace ${roleLabel} est activé avec toutes les fonctionnalités`,
            ].map((step, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 ${i === 2 ? 'bg-accent/10' : 'bg-primary/10'}`}>
                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold ${i === 2 ? 'text-accent' : 'text-primary'}`}>{i + 1}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground pt-1">{step}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>

        <div className="flex justify-center">
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => base44.auth.logout()} className="gap-2">
            <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" /> Se déconnecter
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 117/123: /src/pages/Register.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { UserPlus, Mail, Loader2, Phone, Building2, KeyRound, Store } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import PasswordInput from "@/components/ui/password-input";
import GoogleIcon from "@/components/GoogleIcon";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import PathwaySelection from "@/components/auth/PathwaySelection";
import RegisterOtpStep from "@/components/auth/RegisterOtpStep";

const ROLE_CONFIG = {
  entrepreneur: { label: 'Annonceur', needsAdminValidation: true },
  merchant: { label: 'Commerçant', needsAdminValidation: true },
  citizen: { label: 'Citoyen', needsAdminValidation: false },
};

const generateAffiliationCode = (firstName) => {
  const prefix = (firstName || 'P').slice(0, 3).toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, 'P');
  const random = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
  return `${prefix}${random}`;
};

export default function Register() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState("selection");
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState(null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState("");
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState("");
  const [companyPhone, setCompanyPhone] = useState("");
  const [affiliationCode, setAffiliationCode] = useState(""); // citizen: code from merchant
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState("");
  const [merchantAffiliator, setMerchantAffiliator] = useState(null); // merchant user who owns the code

  // Handle ?ref= (affiliation link from merchant) and ?role= (quick test) query params
  const refCode = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("ref");
  const roleParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("role");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (refCode) {
      // Pre-fill affiliation code and go directly to citizen form
      setAffiliationCode(refCode);
      setSelectedRole("citizen");
      setStep("form");
    } else if (roleParam && ROLE_CONFIG[roleParam]) {
      // Quick test access: pre-select role and go to form
      setSelectedRole(roleParam);
      setStep("form");
    }
  }, [refCode, roleParam]);

  const handleSelectRole = (role) => {
    setSelectedRole(role);
    setError("");
    setStep("form");
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    setStep("selection");
    setSelectedRole(null);
    setError("");
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas");
      return;
    }

    // Validate affiliation code for citizens via publicly-readable ReferralCode entity
    if (selectedRole === "citizen") {
      if (!affiliationCode) {
        setError("Le code d'affiliation commerçant est obligatoire.");
        return;
      }
      try {
        const codes = await base44.entities.ReferralCode.filter({ code: affiliationCode });
        if (codes.length === 0) {
          setError("Code d'affiliation invalide. Demandez le code à votre commerçant.");
          return;
        }
        const activeCode = codes.find(c => c.status === 'active') || codes[0];
        setMerchantAffiliator({ id: activeCode.owner_id });
      } catch {
        setError("Impossible de valider le code d'affiliation.");
        return;
      }
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.register({ email, password });
      setStep("otp");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Échec de l'inscription");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await base44.auth.verifyOtp({ email, otpCode });
      if (result?.access_token) {
        base44.auth.setToken(result.access_token);
      }

      const isMerchant = selectedRole === "merchant";
      const isCitizen = selectedRole === "citizen";

      // Generate affiliation code for merchants so they can affiliate citizens
      const newAffiliationCode = isMerchant ? generateAffiliationCode(firstName) : null;

      const profileData = {
        requested_role: selectedRole,
        first_name: firstName,
        last_name: lastName,
        phone: phone || null,
        company_name: companyName || null,
        company_phone: companyPhone || null,
        affiliation_code: newAffiliationCode,
        affiliated_by: isCitizen && merchantAffiliator ? merchantAffiliator.id : null,
        account_status: (selectedRole === 'entrepreneur' || selectedRole === 'merchant') ? "pending_validation" : "active",
      };

      let meId = null;
      try {
        const me = await base44.auth.me();
        meId = me.id;
        await base44.entities.User.update(me.id, profileData);
      } catch {}

      try {
        await base44.auth.updateMe(profileData);
      } catch {}

      // Create a publicly-readable ReferralCode so citizens can validate the affiliation code
      if (isMerchant && newAffiliationCode && meId) {
        try {
          const existing = await base44.entities.ReferralCode.filter({ code: newAffiliationCode });
          if (existing.length === 0) {
            await base44.entities.ReferralCode.create({
              code: newAffiliationCode,
              owner_id: meId,
              owner_role: 'merchant',
              owner_name: companyName || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim(),
              status: 'active',
            });
          }
        } catch {}
      }

      // Create Referral link: citizen → merchant (enables affiliation gains on watch)
      if (isCitizen && merchantAffiliator && meId) {
        try {
          const existingRef = await base44.entities.Referral.filter({ referred_id: meId });
          if (existingRef.length === 0) {
            await base44.entities.Referral.create({
              referrer_id: merchantAffiliator.id,
              referred_id: meId,
              referral_code: affiliationCode,
              status: 'active',
            });
          }
        } catch {}
      }

      window.location.href = "/";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Code de vérification invalide");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    setError("");
    try {
      await base44.auth.resendOtp(email);
      toast({ title: "Code envoyé", description: "Vérifiez votre email pour le nouveau code." });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Échec du renvoi du code");
    }
  };

  const handleGoogle = () => {
    base44.auth.loginWithProvider("google", "/");
  };

  if (step === "otp") {
    return (
      <RegisterOtpStep
        email={email}
        otpCode={otpCode}
        setOtpCode={setOtpCode}
        error={error}
        loading={loading}
        onVerify={handleVerify}
        onResend={handleResend}
      />
    );
  }

  if (step === "selection") {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={UserPlus}
        title="Inscription P-interact"
        subtitle="Choisissez votre voie d'inscription"
        footer={
          <>
            Déjà un compte ?{" "}
            <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
              Se connecter
            </Link>
          </>
        }
      >
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        <PathwaySelection onSelect={handleSelectRole} />
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const config = ROLE_CONFIG[selectedRole];
  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={UserPlus}
      title={`Inscription ${config?.label || ''}`}
      subtitle="Remplissez vos coordonnées"
      footer={
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <button onClick={handleBack} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
            ← Retour
          </button>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">·</span>
          <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
            Se connecter
          </Link>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        className="w-full h-12 text-sm font-medium mb-6"
        onClick={handleGoogle}
      >
        <GoogleIcon className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
        Continuer avec Google
      </Button>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full border-t border-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase">
          <span className="bg-card px-3 text-muted-foreground">ou</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        {/* Name fields */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="firstName">Prénom</Label>
            <Input id="firstName" value={firstName} onChange={(e) => setFirstName(e.target.value)} className="h-12" required />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="lastName">Nom</Label>
            <Input id="lastName" value={lastName} onChange={(e) => setLastName(e.target.value)} className="h-12" required />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Entrepreneur: company info */}
        {selectedRole === "entrepreneur" && (
          <>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="companyName">Nom de l'entreprise</Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Building2 className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input id="companyName" value={companyName} onChange={(e) => setCompanyName(e.target.value)} className="pl-10 h-12" required />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="companyPhone">Téléphone de l'entreprise</Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Phone className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input id="companyPhone" type="tel" value={companyPhone} onChange={(e) => setCompanyPhone(e.target.value)} className="pl-10 h-12" placeholder="+216 ..." required />
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* Merchant: company + phone */}
        {selectedRole === "merchant" && (
          <>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="companyName">Nom du commerce</Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Store className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input id="companyName" value={companyName} onChange={(e) => setCompanyName(e.target.value)} className="pl-10 h-12" required />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="phone">Téléphone mobile</Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Phone className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input id="phone" type="tel" value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} className="pl-10 h-12" placeholder="+216 ..." required />
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* Citizen: mobile phone + affiliation code */}
        {selectedRole === "citizen" && (
          <>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="phone">Numéro de mobile</Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Phone className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input id="phone" type="tel" value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} className="pl-10 h-12" placeholder="+216 ..." required />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="affiliationCode">Code d'affiliation commerçant</Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <KeyRound className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="affiliationCode"
                  value={affiliationCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setAffiliationCode(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10 h-12 font-mono"
                  placeholder="Code reçu de votre commerçant"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Code fourni par le commerçant qui vous a invité à rejoindre P-interact
              </p>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* Email + password */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">Adresse email</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input id="email" type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className="pl-10 h-12" placeholder="you@example.com" required />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="password">Mot de passe</Label>
          <PasswordInput id="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="••••••••" required />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="confirm">Confirmer le mot de passe</Label>
          <PasswordInput id="confirm" autoComplete="new-password" value={confirmPassword} onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="••••••••" required />
        </div>

        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? <><Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />Création du compte...</> : "Créer un compte"}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 118/123: /src/pages/ResetPassword.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Lock, Loader2, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import PasswordInput from "@/components/ui/password-input";

export default function ResetPassword() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const resetToken = searchParams.get("token");

  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPassword({ resetToken, newPassword });
      window.location.href = "/login";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Échec de la réinitialisation");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!resetToken) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={AlertTriangle}
        title="Lien invalide"
        subtitle="Ce lien de réinitialisation est manquant ou invalide"
        footer={
          <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
            Demander un nouveau lien
          </Link>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          Le lien utilisé semble incomplet. Veuillez demander un nouveau lien de réinitialisation.
        </p>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Lock}
      title="Nouveau mot de passe"
      subtitle="Entrez votre nouveau mot de passe"
    >
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="password">Nouveau mot de passe</Label>
          <PasswordInput
            id="password"
            autoComplete="new-password"
            autoFocus
            placeholder="••••••••"
            value={newPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="confirm">Confirmer le mot de passe</Label>
          <PasswordInput
            id="confirm"
            autoComplete="new-password"
            placeholder="••••••••"
            value={confirmPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Réinitialisation...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Réinitialiser"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FICHIER 119/123: /src/pages/UserGuidePage.jsx
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { BookOpen, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { ROLE_GUIDES } from '@/components/shared/RoleOnboarding';

export default function UserGuidePage() {
  const { data: user } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['me'],
    queryFn: () => base44.auth.me(),
  });

  const userRole = user?.role || 'citizen';
  const requestedRole = user?.requested_role;
  const effectiveRole = requestedRole && requestedRole !== userRole ? requestedRole : userRole;
  const guide = ROLE_GUIDES[effectiveRole] || ROLE_GUIDES.citizen;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/10 to-accent/10 border border-primary/20">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-primary/10">
            <BookOpen className="w-8 h-8 text-primary" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-black">{guide.title}</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">
              Guide d'usage Toointeract — découvrez les fonctionnalités de votre espace
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-5">
        <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-3">Fonctionnalités</h2>
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {guide.features.map((f, i) => {
            const Icon = f.icon;
            return (
              <div key={i} className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-muted/20 border border-border">
                <Icon className={`w-5 h-5 ${guide.color} mb-2`} />
                <p className="text-sm font-bold">{f.title}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{f.desc}</p>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Card>

      <Card className="p-5">
        <h2 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-3">Instructions du programme</h2>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {guide.steps.map((step, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-card ${guide.color} flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5`}>
                {i + 1}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground pt-1">{step}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-accent/5 border border-accent/20 flex items-center gap-2">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-accent flex-shrink-0" />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Pour toute question, consultez ce guide ou contactez le support Toointeract.
          </p>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

export default function Welcome() {

        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto text-center space-y-8">
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary text-xs font-medium">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {t('welcome.tagline')}
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-heading font-bold tracking-tight">

                <Link to="/login">{t('welcome.login')}</Link>

              </div>
              <h3 className="font-heading font-semibold mb-1">{t('welcome.password')}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-4">{t('welcome.reset_access')}</p>
              <Button asChild className="w-full" size="sm" variant="ghost">
                <Link to="/forgot-password">{t('welcome.forgot')}</Link>
              </Button>

          </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-4">

                  <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground text-center mb-3">{row.desc}</p>
                  <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="w-full">
                    <Link to={`/register?role=${row.role}`}>{t('welcome.signup')}</Link>
                  </Button>
                </Card>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Features */}
          <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 pt-8 border-t border-border/50">
            {[
              { icon: ScanLine, label: t('welcome.scan_qr'), desc: t('welcome.scan_qr_desc') },
              { icon: Wallet, label: t('welcome.gains_dt'), desc: t('welcome.gains_dt_desc') },
              { icon: ShieldCheck, label: t('welcome.face_match'), desc: t('welcome.face_match_desc') },

                <f.icon className="w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground" />

                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{f.desc}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <footer className="border-t border-border/50 py-4 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        {t('welcome.footer')}
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

export function createPageUrl(pageName: string) {
    return '/' + pageName.replace(/ /g, '-');
}
